Experiments on LasHeR, and zero-shot evaluations on RGBT234 and GTOT under multiple cross-modal protocols demonstrate improvements over representative state-of-the-art trackers. For instance, under the modality-switch protocol on RGBT234, Pre-AFA TSDA-Track achieves an SR/PR of 43.2/56.0, compared with 36.8/50.0 for ToMP-101 baseline. In addition, a study on Anti-UAV-024 further verifies the applicability of TSDA-Track to aerial tracking. Our study highlights the effectiveness of feature alignment domain adaptation for cross-modal tracking.

</details>


### [37] [Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving Agent with Language-based Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38641)
*Kai Yan,Xiangyu Chen,Yulong Cao,Alex Naumann,Peter Karkus,Yan Wang,Jef Packer,Alex Schwing,Yuxiong Wang,Boris Ivanovic,Wenjie Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AD-Memo 提出一种以自然语言作为记忆的通用 VLA 驾驶智能体，通过将记忆作为 CoT 的延伸并采用 SFT + Da Capo 半闭环强化学习训练，提升长时序驾驶理解与驾驶质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 基础模型因图像 token 成本高而只能输入有限帧，难以应对依赖记忆的任务（如全向停车到达顺序判断、长时序场景理解）。现有潜在向量记忆通过交叉注意力访问，既不可解释也不易迁移。

Method: 提出 AD-Memo，将记忆以语言形式作为 Chain-of-Thought 的扩展输出，记录对驾驶关键的环境物体，并作为未来输入的一部分；构建记忆数据集；采用两阶段训练：监督微调（SFT）与 Da Capo —— 一种新颖的半闭环 RL 算法，对记忆使用轨迹级优势、对驾驶使用步级优势以改善信用分配。

Result: 在全向停车和一般驾驶等场景中，AD-Memo 提升了驾驶质量，改善了驾驶场景问答表现，并能为其他模型生成即插即用的记忆。

Conclusion: 语言化、可解释且可迁移的记忆机制结合半闭环 RL 训练，是提升 VLA 自动驾驶长时序记忆能力的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently emerged as one of the prevailing solutions for autonomous driving, as they can utilize knowledge acquired during vision-language pretraining for accurate and interpretable driving. However, VLAs can take only a limited number of frames as visual input due to the high token cost of an image, which is problematic for memory-dependent tasks such as determining the arrival order at all-way stops and long-horizon driving scene understanding. Existing solutions use latent vector memories accessed through cross-attention, which are neither interpretable nor portable. In this paper, we propose AD-Memo, a general-purpose VLA driving agent with language-based memory. The agent outputs memory as an extension of its Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to record surrounding objects critical to driving; this memory becomes part of the agent's future input. We curate memory-based datasets and train VLAs with a two-stage recipe: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and \textit{Da Capo}, a novel semi-closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm which uses trajectory-level advantage for memory and step-level advantage for driving, leading to better credit assignment. Across scenarios such as all-way stops and general driving, AD-Memo improves driving quality, enables better question answering on driving scenes, and produces plug-and-play memory for other models.

</details>


### [38] [ReGain: Restoring Subject Fidelity in Personalization on Synthetic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38680)
*Shubhang Bhatnagar,Ishan Bhatnagar,Viraj Shah,Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现用扩散模型生成的合成图像做DreamBooth个性化微调会降低主体保真度（过饱和、高频细节过多），并将原因追溯到无分类器引导（CFG）的差异膨胀，提出无需真实照片的采样期校正方法ReGain。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的个性化图像来自扩散模型而非相机，作者想探究用合成图像做DreamBooth微调为何会损害主体保真度，并找出其根本原因。

Method: 用相同基础模型和相同DreamBooth配方分别对真实照片和合成图像微调两个模型进行对比；定位问题源于CFG（条件与无条件噪声预测夹角及差值范数被放大，且偏向高频）。提出ReGain：一种训练期无需真实照片的采样期校正方法，测量各频段引导相对基础模型的膨胀程度并相应缩放。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.5上，ReGain以DINO、DINOv2、CLIP-I衡量，弥补了51-64%与真实照片模型的保真度差距；在SDXL和SD 3.5上同样提升主体保真度，并在三种骨干上保持文本对齐。

Conclusion: 合成图像微调导致的保真度退化可通过分析CFG引导膨胀来诊断，ReGain作为无需真实照片的采样期校正能有效恢复主体保真度且保持文本一致性。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are personalized to a subject by DreamBooth fine-tuning on a handful of its images. Increasingly, these images come from a diffusion model rather than a camera. We show that fine-tuning on such synthetic images degrades subject fidelity, producing oversaturated color and excess high-frequency detail. To isolate the cause, we fine-tune two models from the same base model with the same DreamBooth recipe, one on real photos of a subject and one on synthetic images of that subject generated by the first. We trace the degradation to classifier-free guidance (CFG). For the model personalized on synthetic images, the angle between the conditional and unconditional noise predictions, and with it the norm of their difference, is much larger than for the model personalized on real photos. This inflation grows toward high frequencies and also appears at other prompts semantically close to the subject, such as its class noun, but not at unrelated ones. We propose ReGain, a training-free correction applied at sampling time that measures how much each frequency band of the guidance is inflated relative to the base model and scales that band down accordingly. ReGain needs no real photos. On Stable Diffusion v1.5, ReGain closes 51-64% of the subject-fidelity gap to the model personalized on real photos, as measured by DINO, DINOv2 and CLIP-I. It also improves subject fidelity on SDXL and SD 3.5 and preserves text alignment on all three backbones.

</details>


### [39] [Unveiling the Value of Motion for Cinematic Camera Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38683)
*Ziqi Zhou,Yujian Yuan,Laura Sevilla-Lara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将相机轨迹表示从传统的逐帧姿态分解为方向与速度，发现该简单表示在轨迹-文本对齐和文本-轨迹生成任务上均带来显著提升，并提出CineGEN生成模型与CineScript数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影运镜是核心叙事工具，不仅取决于相机在场景中的位置，还取决于运动的方向与速度。现有相机轨迹生成与文本对齐工作依赖以姿态（pose）为中心的表示。理论上网络可以从姿态中推导方向与速度，但作者发现实践中并非如此，因此需要更有效的轨迹表示方式。

Method: 作者将相机轨迹表示从传统的逐帧姿态分解为方向（direction）和速度（speed）；针对轨迹到文本的对齐，提出一种简单可靠的评估协议以克服现有基线的局限；针对文本到轨迹生成，基于该表示洞见提出生成模型CineGEN；同时构建CineScript数据集，包含带有场景描述和更高层元数据的电影片段。

Result: 方向与速度的表示在轨迹-文本对齐和文本-轨迹生成任务上均取得更好的数值表现；CineGEN在多项指标上达到更优性能；CineScript数据集使模型能够被测试其捕获高层电影语言信息的能力；该表示还能内在编码复杂的导演意图。

Conclusion: 尽管表示形式简单，用方向与速度表示相机轨迹不仅能提升对齐与生成的量化指标，还能天然编码复杂的导演意图，为相机轨迹理解与生成提供了更有效的表示基础。

Abstract: Cinematic camera motion is a fundamental storytelling tool, defined not only by where the camera is positioned in the scene, but also by how it moves in terms of direction and speed. Recent work on camera trajectory generation and alignment to text relies on pose-centric representations. While in principle a network could derive direction of movement and speed, we find that in practice this might not happen. In fact, in this paper we discover that decomposing the camera trajectory representation from the traditional per-frame poses to direction and speed has surprising benefits across multiple tasks, including trajectory-to-text alignment as well as text-to-trajectory generation. To accurately evaluate the former, we introduce a simple and reliable protocol that overcomes the limitations of prior evaluation baselines. For the latter, building on this representational insight, we propose a novel generative model for camera trajectories, CineGEN, that achieves superior performance across a variety of metrics. We also propose a novel dataset, CineScript, containing movie clips that are enriched with scene descriptions as well as higher-level metadata. This novel data allows us to test models' ability to capture high-level cinematographic information. We show that, despite its simplicity, representing camera trajectories through direction and speed not only helps numerically to achieve better alignment and generation, but also inherently encodes complex directorial intent.

</details>


### [40] [EPIC: Epipolar-Consistent 360° Immersive Stereo Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38689)
*Debabrata Mandal,Dongdong Fu,Jonathon Miller,William Villareal,Xi Peng,Praneeth Chakravarthula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本生成流程，将现有视频扩散模型扩展为4K立体360°视频，用极线感知匹配度量作为偏好信号进行直接偏好优化，实现沉浸式显示的内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式显示需要丰富多样的虚拟体验，但手动制作所有体验成本高昂、难以扩展且不切实际。现有生成式AI模型是为传统显示设计的，无法生成沉浸式观看所需的高分辨率、立体360°内容；此外，传统显示上可容忍的时间与立体不一致性在沉浸式头显中会变得极具干扰性。

Method: 受双目视觉和深度感知启发，开发了极线感知的360°图像匹配度量，用于捕捉不同视图间的时间与立体几何不一致性；将该度量作为偏好信号，在有限训练数据下进行直接偏好优化，构建零样本生成流程，将现有视频扩散模型扩展为4K立体360°视频。

Result: 实现了360°立体视频生成，并提供了将生成式内容带入沉浸式显示的可扩展路径，可按需支持多样化的混合现实体验。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了现有生成模型无法生成沉浸式高分辨率立体360°内容的空白，为沉浸式显示中的生成式内容提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Immersive displays can enable rich and diverse virtual experiences. Manually authoring every possible experience to realize this potential, however, is prohibitively expensive, difficult to scale, and impractical. Generative AI models could remove this bottleneck, but today's models are built for conventional displays and cannot generate the high-resolution, stereoscopic $360^\circ$ content required for immersive viewing. Further, temporal and stereo inconsistencies that may be tolerable on conventional displays can become highly disruptive when viewed through an immersive headset.
  Here, we address this gap with a zero-shot generative pipeline that extends existing video diffusion models into 4K stereoscopic $360^\circ$ videos. Inspired from binocular vision and depth perception, we develop an epipolar-aware $360^\circ$ image matching metric that captures the temporal and stereo geometric inconsistencies across views. We then use this metric as a preference signal for direct preference optimization with limited training data. Our work enables $360^\circ$ stereo video generation and provides a scalable path for bringing generative content to immersive displays, allowing diverse mixed reality experiences on demand.

</details>


### [41] [No Corners Cut: State-Grounded Transitions for Mid-Stream Prompt Switches in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38691)
*Zejing Rao,Ketong Ren,Xiaoqiang Liu,Yiping Meng,Guoxin Zhang,Fan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEGUE框架，通过训练无关规划器生成过渡提示，并用SPANDMD训练策略，使流式视频生成在提示切换时执行忠实的中间状态过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频生成方法在响应中途切换的指令时倾向于偷工减料，过早实现目标或走启发式捷径，跳过了合理过渡所需的中间状态变化。

Method: SEGUE在每次切换时，由训练无关的规划器解析最新帧与提示，写出若干带角色与时长的segue提示，再交还给用户提示。同时提出SPANDMD：以完整rollout作为时间上下文评估每个激活提示，但仅在该提示的指定时间跨度内保留其DMD残差，以解决短时时间表上训练因果模型的困难。

Result: 在包含1,800次切换的OpenTrans-360基准上，SEGUE在全部八项过渡指标上排名第一，整体分数由最强基线的0.866提升至0.887。在StreamAV-Bench的六项指令响应指标中四项排名第一，且规划器无需再训练即可迁移至冻结的自回归生成器。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并训练过渡过程，SEGUE实现了忠实的流式视频状态转换，兼具性能优势与跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Streaming video generators allow users to dynamically modulate video synthesis via mid-stream prompt switching. Existing streaming methods can respond to the updated instruction while still cutting corners, prematurely realizing goals or taking heuristic shortcuts that bypass necessary intermediate state changes needed for a plausible transition. In this study, we present SEGUE, a novel framework that makes this process explicit and trains the generator to execute these transitions faithfully. At each switch, a training-free planner parses the latest frame and prompts, writes a few segue prompts with roles and durations, and then hands control back to the user's prompt. Furthermore, to address the inherent difficulty of training causal models on short-lived temporal schedules without corrupting preparatory supervision, we introduce SPANDMD, which evaluates each active prompt using the full rollout as temporal context while retaining its DMD residual only within the prompt's assigned span. On OpenTrans-360, a benchmark of 1,800 switches that scores how the old state exits and the new one begins, SEGUE ranks first on all eight transition metrics and raises the overall score over the strongest baseline from 0.866 to 0.887. It also ranks first on four of six instruction-response metrics of StreamAV-Bench, while the planner transfers to frozen autoregressive generators without retraining.

</details>


### [42] [SCALE: Synthetic Calibration via Agreement Labeling in Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38705)
*Wenjun Liu,Saeed Hassanpour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究病理基础模型的校准问题，发现其在专家一致性低的困难样本上校准误差更高，并提出无需多标注者标签的合成一致性校准方法，在多个数据集上改善校准且保持判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型通常只用AUC和准确率评估，校准常被忽略。模型即便平均准确率不错，也可能对连病理学家都难以判断的困难病例给出过度自信的概率，因此需要研究校准，尤其是困难样本上的校准表现。

Method: 研究八个病理基础模型的校准，以病理学家一致性衡量诊断难度。提出合成一致性校准：在训练好的线性探针上，选取高置信度嵌入作为类别锚点，在相反类别的锚点间插值，插值权重编码连续诊断模糊性，作为合成一致性信号，并结合一致性感知的标签平滑重新训练探针。

Result: 校准误差在低一致性病例上一致高于高一致性病例，且这一现象无法仅从整体ECE看出。在含七位病理学家标注的MHIST上，合成一致性校准恢复了基于真实一致性标签平滑的大部分校准改进，同时相比未校准基线大幅降低低一致性ECE，且判别指标保持。在无多标注者标签的PatchCamelyon和BreakHis上，该方法在各基础模型上均改善校准。

Conclusion: 病理基础模型的校准应特别关注困难、低一致性样本。合成一致性校准无需额外专家标注即可改善校准，在有多标注和无多标注数据集上均有效，同时保持判别能力。

Abstract: Foundation models for computational pathology are usually evaluated using AUC and accuracy, while calibration is often left untested. This matters because a model can be accurate on average but still assign overly confident probabilities to cases that are difficult even for pathologists. We study calibration across eight pathology foundation models. Using pathologist agreement as a measure of diagnostic difficulty, we find that calibration error is consistently higher on low-agreement cases than on high-agreement cases. This pattern is not apparent from aggregate expected calibration error (ECE) alone. We then propose synthetic agreement calibration, a method for improving calibration without collecting multi-annotator labels. Given a trained linear probe, we select high-confidence embeddings as class anchors and interpolate between anchors from opposite classes. The interpolation weights encode a continuous notion of diagnostic ambiguity, which we use as a synthetic agreement signal to retrain the probe with agreement-aware label smoothing. On MHIST, which includes annotations from seven pathologists, synthetic agreement calibration recovers most of the calibration improvement obtained by label smoothing based on real pathologist agreement, while substantially reducing low-agreement ECE relative to the uncalibrated baseline. Discrimination metrics are preserved. On PatchCamelyon and BreakHis, public histopathology datasets without multi-annotator labels, the method improves calibration across the evaluated foundation models, whereas annotator-dependent approaches cannot be used without additional expert annotation.

</details>


### [43] [Hard-Region Supervision: #1 on the Waymo Open Dataset 2D Video Panoptic Segmentation Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38714)
*Jinghan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是Waymo开放数据集2D视频全景分割挑战赛的获胜方案，在DVIS++基线上提出硬区域监督（HRS）及三项测试时改进，取得三项指标均第一的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该任务要求对每帧每个像素给出语义类别，并对可数物体在100帧和五个重叠相机间保持身份一致。作者希望在此挑战中取得领先成绩，并提升基线模型在困难区域的性能。

Method: 以DVIS++（分割器、跟踪器、精化器的级联结构）为基线。提出硬区域监督（HRS）：利用基线定义其出错的困难区域，为该区域设计损失函数和辅助预测头，辅助头仅用于训练、测试时移除，故推理时模型结构与基线一致。另提出三种测试时步骤：双模型集成、将分割器输出合并进最终全景图、跨相机身份关联。

Result: 在挑战测试集上，参赛方案达到0.3547 wSTQ、0.2071 wAQ、0.6075 mIoU，三项指标均排名第一；比第二名高出3.6 wSTQ点，比DVIS++基线高出2.4点。

Conclusion: 硬区域监督能在不改变推理结构的前提下提升性能，结合三项测试时改进可显著增强视频全景分割效果，最终在挑战中夺冠。

Abstract: We describe our winning entry to the Waymo Open Dataset 2D Video Panoptic Segmentation Challenge. The task asks for a semantic class at every pixel of every frame and, for countable objects, an identity that holds across 100 frames and across five overlapping cameras. We build on DVIS++, a cascade of a segmenter, a tracker, and a refiner, as our baseline. We propose hard region supervision (HRS) to improve the baseline. In particular, we use the baseline to define the hard region as where it makes mistakes, and design a loss and an auxiliary prediction head for this region. The auxiliary head is used only in training and removed at test time, so at inference the model trained with HRS has the same architecture as the baseline. In addition, we propose three test-time steps that further improve the results: a two-model ensemble, a merge of the segmenter's output into the final panoptic map, and cross-camera identity linking. On the challenge test set, our entry reaches 0.3547 wSTQ, 0.2071 wAQ, and 0.6075 mIoU, ranking first on all three metrics. It is 3.6 wSTQ points ahead of the second entry and 2.4 points ahead of our DVIS++ baseline.

</details>


### [44] [SpatialCORE: Confidence-Aware Grounded Spatial Reasoning in Large Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38716)
*Rafi Ibn Sultan,Xiangyu Zhou,Md. Sajid Alam Chowdhury,Chengyin Li,Prashant Khanduri,Marco Brocanelli,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialCORE 后训练框架，将模型对生成式定位的自身置信度转化为空间推理的学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在视觉感知任务上进展显著，但空间推理仍是持续弱点，尤其是需要基于视觉空间进行推理的问题。近期方法引入生成式定位（预测边界框、掩码等作为推理轨迹的一部分），但通常只优化最终答案正确性，导致即便模型并非基于高置信度定位到的任务相关目标进行推理，也能获得正确答案奖励。

Method: SpatialCORE（Spatially COnfident REasoning）的核心思想是强化既准确又高置信的定位，促使模型基于高置信定位的任务相关目标进行推理。具体通过一个自调节空间奖励实现：用每个预测边界框的坐标 token 置信度对其匹配质量进行加权；并引入答案门控（answer gate），将定位优化与最终答案正确性绑定。

Result: 在多个基准上，SpatialCORE 在开源模型与专门的空间推理模型中取得最先进（state-of-the-art）结果，并在零样本设置下有效迁移到未见数据分布。

Conclusion: 将模型自身定位置信度作为学习信号，可有效提升空间推理能力，并具备良好的泛化性；源代码已公开。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have made remarkable progress across visual perception tasks, yet spatial reasoning remains a persistent weakness, especially for questions that require reasoning over visual space. Recent spatial-reasoning methods incorporate generated grounding, where models predict bounding boxes, masks, or other localization outputs for task-relevant objects as part of their reasoning trace. However, these approaches typically optimize final-answer correctness alone, allowing correct answers to be rewarded even when the model does not reason from confidently localized task-relevant objects. We introduce SpatialCORE (Spatially COnfident REasoning), a post-training framework that turns the model's own confidence in generated grounding into a learning signal for spatial reasoning. Its central idea is to reinforce grounding that is both accurate and confident, encouraging the model to reason from confidently localized task-relevant objects. SpatialCORE realizes this through a self-regulating spatial reward that weights each predicted bounding box's matching quality by its coordinate-token confidence. An answer gate further ties grounding optimization to final-answer correctness. SpatialCORE achieves state-of-the-art results among open-source and specialized spatial reasoning models across diverse benchmarks, and transfers effectively in zero-shot settings to unseen data distributions. The source code is available at https://github.com/rafiibnsultan/SpatialCORE.

</details>


### [45] [Soft Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38717)
*Rafi Ibn Sultan,Md. Sajid Alam Chowdhury,Saleh Zare Zade,Chengyin Li,Prashant Khanduri,Marco Brocanelli,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Soft Spatial Reasoning，一种用于 LVLM 空间推理的后训练框架，通过连续软状态替代硬 token 选择，并用 AdaptSoft 控制器自适应调节软度，在多个空间基准上优于硬 CoT 与固定软度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 通常用 CoT 做空间推理，每一步需在不确定情况下硬性选择一个离散 token，产生"过早离散化"，一旦选错就会在后续推理中传播错误。

Method: 在每个中间推理步骤，通过混合 token 嵌入形成连续软状态而非选择单一 token，使多个候选延续共同影响下一步；核心是 AdaptSoft 控制器，利用当前隐状态和预测不确定性自适应调整每步的软度；并引入梯度对齐学习目标，在无中间推理监督的情况下为软度控制提供步级学习信号。

Result: 在多样化空间基准上，Soft Spatial Reasoning 在相同骨干下优于硬 CoT 和固定软度 CoT 基线，也优于多种现有 LVLM。

Conclusion: 自适应软思考能有效缓解空间推理中的过早离散化问题，提升 LVLM 空间推理性能，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) commonly perform spatial reasoning through chain-of-thought (CoT), encoding intermediate reasoning as autoregressive sequences of discrete language tokens. Such hard thinking requires committing to a single token at each step, even when the correct spatial interpretation remains uncertain. This early commitment constitutes premature discretization: an incorrect token selection can propagate errors through subsequent reasoning. We propose Soft Spatial Reasoning, a post-training framework that introduces soft thinking for spatial tasks in LVLMs. At each intermediate reasoning step, the LVLM forms a continuous soft state by mixing token embeddings rather than selecting a single token, allowing multiple candidate continuations to influence the next step. The appropriate degree of softness, however, can vary across reasoning steps: retaining multiple candidates may preserve a useful spatial interpretation, but if those candidates imply conflicting spatial relations, mixing them may interfere with subsequent reasoning. At the core of Soft Spatial Reasoning is AdaptSoft, a controller that uses the current hidden state and predictive uncertainty to adapt the degree of softness at each reasoning step. To train AdaptSoft, we introduce a gradient-alignment learning objective that provides a step-specific learning signal for softness control without intermediate reasoning supervision. Across diverse spatial benchmarks, Soft Spatial Reasoning outperforms hard and fixed-soft CoT baselines using the same backbone, as well as a range of existing LVLMs. The source code is available at https://github.com/rafiibnsultan/Soft_Spatial_Reasoning

</details>


### [46] [Matisse: Evidence-Space Reasoning for Active 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38746)
*Xihang Yu,Kaichen Zhou,Lorenzo Shaikewitz,Clément Jambon,Xiao Zhan,Rajat Talak,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Matisse是一个免训练框架，利用预训练生成式3D模型的交叉注意力证据估计不确定性，统一主动重建与关键帧选择，降低重建误差并加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动视角获取方法通常只估计已观测或已实例化几何的不确定性，难以推理未见结构；长视野重建方法又常保留冗余观测。在有限计算预算下，如何有效获取并保留有用信息以理解部分视角下的场景几何，是核心问题。

Method: 利用预训练生成式3D模型提供的证据，从与3D潜在token相关的交叉注意力证据中估计Evidential Uncertainty，并推导Evidential Information Gain，基于后验熵的期望减少来指导视角获取和关键帧选择。通过遮挡感知、对象平衡的聚合支持多物体场景，并在中间潜变量中传播不确定性，避免规划时完整重建。框架免训练。

Result: 在GSO30、YCB-V、Replica上，Chamfer距离相对各数据集最佳基线分别降低12.7%、3.8%、9.2%；在GSO30上，相同重建后端下相比最佳主动重建基线实现1.50倍端到端加速。在GSO30长视野重建关键帧选择实验中，仅用14%的输入视角即达到与Stream3D相当的性能。

Conclusion: Matisse通过生成式3D模型的证据驱动不确定性估计，统一了主动重建与关键帧选择，在有限计算下提升几何理解效率与精度，并显著减少冗余观测。

Abstract: How can a 3D reconstruction system acquire and retain useful information to understand the geometry of a scene from partial views under a limited computation budget? Existing active view acquisition methods typically estimate uncertainty over observed or instantiated geometry, limiting their ability to reason about unseen structure, while long-horizon reconstruction methods often retain redundant observations. We introduce Matisse, a training-free framework that unifies active reconstruction and keyframe selection by leveraging evidence provided by a pretrained generative 3D model. Matisse estimates Evidential Uncertainty from cross-attention evidence associated with 3D latent tokens and derives an Evidential Information Gain to guide both view acquisition and keyframe selection based on the expected reduction in posterior entropy. Matisse supports multi-object scenes through occlusion-aware, object-balanced aggregation and propagates uncertainty through intermediate latents to avoid full reconstruction during planning. Matisse reduces Chamfer distance by 12.7%, 3.8%, and 9.2% on GSO30, YCB-V, and Replica, respectively, relative to the best baseline on each dataset, and achieves a $1.50\times$ end-to-end speedup over the best active reconstruction baseline on GSO30 with the same reconstruction backend. In the GSO30 keyframe selection experiment for long-horizon reconstruction, Matisse achieves comparable Chamfer distance using 14% of the input views compared with Stream3D.

</details>


### [47] [Consensus-Aware Multi-Source Fusion for Reference-Guided Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38747)
*Junyang Xia,Luocheng Zhang,Wenwen Pan,Chifeng Zhu,Yang Yang,Xinchun Liu,Jiajun Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出共识感知的多源融合框架，用于参考引导的伪装目标检测，通过参考条件双骨干融合、跨参考共识聚合与分层解码提升分割效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考引导伪装目标检测的目标与背景视觉高度相似，难点在于参考样本中的目标线索不一致，且通用视觉表示与参考指定的目标并不天然对齐。

Method: 提出共识感知多源融合框架。其核心组件RCDF将可训练的PVTv2查询特征与冻结的DINOv3表示耦合，采用参考条件相关性在多层融合前筛选基础模型证据；并通过跨参考共识聚合整合多个参考，同时在语义深度上与查询特征匹配注入参考信息。

Result: 大量实验验证了方法有效性；结果显示参考共识、目标条件化的基础模型特征与分层解码在评测协议下带来互补提升。

Conclusion: 参考共识、目标条件基础特征和分层解码是互补的改进来源，所提框架能有效应对参考线索不一致与表示不对齐问题，代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Reference-guided camouflaged object detection aims to segment a target whose visual appearance closely resembles its surroundings by exploiting auxiliary reference samples. The task remains difficult because reference samples contain inconsistent target cues, while generic visual representations are not inherently aligned with the target specified by the references. To handle these problems, we present a consensus-aware multi-source fusion framework. Reference-Conditioned Dual-Backbone Fusion (RCDF) couples trainable PVTv2 query features with frozen DINOv3 representations and uses reference-conditioned correlation to select foundation-model evidence before multi-scale fusion. The framework also aggregates multiple references through cross-reference consensus aggregation and injects reference information at semantic depths matched to the query features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The results further show that reference consensus, target-conditioned foundation features, and hierarchical decoding provide complementary improvements under the evaluation protocol. The source code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [48] [Here the World in Stereo: Learning Dynamic Spatial Correspondence for Immersive Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38748)
*Hanmo Chen,Chengcheng Liu,Tianxiao Chen,Zheyu Zhang,Siming Zheng,Jinwei Chen,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng,Bo Li,Peng-tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 StereoBind 框架，将视觉源运动与立体声音频生成绑定，以实现动态空间对应，并构建了配套数据集与评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合视频-音频生成模型虽具备较好的语义对应与时序同步，但 AR/VR、互动游戏等应用需要的立体声音频被忽视。立体声要求感知声源位置随对应视觉源运动一致变化，作者将此性质定义为动态空间对应（Dynamic Spatial Correspondence），据此提出研究问题。

Method: 提出 StereoBind 框架：用运动轨迹协调视觉运动与立体声音频，通过三种互补机制实现——视觉运动绑定建立源感知的音视频对应，空间轨迹编码器捕获声源绝对位置，残差轨迹 RoPE 建模相对运动。同时构建了含配对运动轨迹的大规模立体声音视频数据集 StereoWorld-29K，以及评测音视频空间一致性的 StereoWorldBench。

Result: 实验表明，StereoBind 在立体声音频生成的空间对齐方面显著优于现有模型，同时保持整体音视频质量。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉源运动与立体声生成绑定，StereoBind 有效提升了动态空间对应能力，为沉浸式音视频生成提供了数据、方法与评测基准。

Abstract: Recent joint video-audio generation models have achieved strong semantic correspondence and temporal synchronization. However, applications such as AR/VR and interactive gaming further require stereo audio to provide an immersive sense, which remains largely overlooked. Effective stereo audio requires the perceived sound location to evolve consistently with the motion of its corresponding visual source. We refer to this property as Dynamic Spatial Correspondence and propose StereoBind, a framework that binds visual source motion to stereo sound generation. StereoBind uses motion tracks to coordinate visual motion and stereo audio through three complementary mechanisms. Visual Motion Binding establishes source-aware audiovisual correspondence, the Spatial Track Encoder captures absolute source positions, and Residual Track RoPE models relative motion. For supervision and evaluation, we construct StereoWorld-29K, a large-scale stereo audio-video dataset with paired motion tracks, and StereoWorldBench for measuring audiovisual spatial consistency. Experiments show that StereoBind substantially improves spatial alignment in stereo audio generation over existing models while preserving overall audiovisual quality.

</details>


### [49] [Agentic Relative Camera Pose Estimation via Learned Ranking and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38755)
*Zhining Gu,Shangjie Du,Weimin Qiu,Carl Olsson,Ping Liu,Meng Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoseAgent是一个智能体框架，通过可学习的排名与验证来动态调度多种相机位姿估计器，在多个基准上提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同相机位姿估计方法（对应匹配、端到端回归、3D几何基础模型）各有优势，没有单一估计器在所有挑战（大基线、弱纹理、外观变化、遮挡）下都最优，且在不同基准和图像对间性能波动大、互补性强。

Method: 给定图像对，先由profiling agent提取外观、语义与几何特征（如场景类型）；再由学习的ranking agent预测多个估计器的相对能力；执行排名最高的估计器，并用学习的verification agent预测位姿误差进行验证；若失败则自适应调用排名更低的估计器，直到候选被接受或达到执行预算。

Result: 验证网络预测位姿误差比先前模型更准；在ARKitScenes、MegaDepth、ScanNet++和RealEstate10K上，AUC@5度相比最强单一估计器最高提升4.2%；在ARKitScenes上还优于VLM-based agents（使用相同验证器与回退策略的VLM排序器）。

Conclusion: 结果表明所提出的学习式排名与验证机制的有效性，动态编排多估计器能显著提升相对相机位姿估计性能。

Abstract: A wide range of approaches have been developed for camera pose estimation, including correspondence-based methods, end-to-end pose regression, and recent 3D geometric foundation models. Our key observation is that no single estimator is optimal for diverse challenges, such as wide baselines, lack of texture, appearance changes, and occlusions. Further analysis reveals substantial performance variation across both benchmarks and individual image pairs, with different estimators exhibiting complementary strengths. We introduce PoseAgent, an agentic framework for relative camera pose estimation that dynamically orchestrates pose estimators through learnable ranking and verification. Given an image pair, a profiling agent first extracts appearance, semantic, and geometric features relevant to pose estimation, e.g., scene type. A learned ranking agent then predicts the relative competence of multiple pose estimators given the image-pair profile. The top-ranked estimator is executed, and its predicted pose is assessed by a learned verification agent that estimates the corresponding pose error. When verification fails, PoseAgent adaptively invokes lower-ranked estimators until a candidate is accepted or the execution budget is reached. For pose verification, our verification network predicts pose errors more accurately than prior models. For pose estimation, PoseAgent improves AUC@5 degree up to 4.2% over the strongest standalone estimator on each of ARKitScenes, MegaDepth, ScanNet++, and RealEstate10K. On ARKitScenes, PoseAgent also outperforms VLM-based agents, which include a VLM ranker with the same verifier and fallback policy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of our learned ranking and verification.

</details>


### [50] [Event-Driven Refresh and Recurrence Memory to Reduce Stale Grounding in Referring Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38758)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Jaehyun Jang,Siwoo Lim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EDRRM方法，通过事件驱动的刷新与循环记忆增强Sa2VA的指代视频物体分割，在降低刷新调用成本的同时保持或提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sa2VA这类方法仅在少量初始关键帧上做指代定位，之后依赖传播。在长视频或动态视频中，物体组成发生变化（干扰物进入、目标消失/重现）时会导致过时的定位和持续性的假阳性。

Method: EDRRM是一种选择性增强策略：仅在稳定的变化点重新调用Sa2VA。用基于跟踪线索（目标生灭、粗糙的组成/布局变化）计算的EMA平滑事件分数配合时间约束来确定刷新边界；并用循环记忆通过CLIP相似度检索锚帧，在目标重现事件时重新为模型提供条件。

Result: 在Ref-DAVIS17、MeViS和ReVOS上，EDRRM相比固定窗口和FrameDiff-SSIM基线实现了有竞争力的精度-效率权衡，在显著更低的平均刷新调用预算下保持相当或更优的J&F分数，并减少了假阳性失败。端到端运行时分析显示，跟踪、CLIP循环匹配和可识别性门控带来的额外开销相对Sa2VA推理主成本较为有限。

Conclusion: EDRRM验证了选择性刷新与循环记忆机制的有效性和效率，在精度与计算成本之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Referring Video Object Segmentation (RVOS) aims to produce a pixel-accurate mask sequence for an object specified by natural language. Sa2VA combines a multimodal large language model with SAM2 for grounded segmentation; however, its inference typically grounds the query from a small fixed set of initial keyframes and then relies on propagation. In long or dynamic videos, this can cause stale grounding and persistent false positives when the object composition changes (e.g., distractors enter or the target disappears/re-appears). We propose Event-Driven Refresh + Recurrence Memory (EDRRM), an enhancement that selectively re-invokes Sa2VA only at stable change points. EDRRM triggers refresh boundaries using an EMA-smoothed event score computed from tracking-derived cues (births/deaths and coarse composition/layout changes) with temporal constraints. A recurrence memory further retrieves anchor frames via CLIP similarity to re-condition the model on re-appearance events. Experiments on Ref-DAVIS17, MeViS, and ReVOS show that EDRRM achieves a competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-off relative to fixed-window and FrameDiff-SSIM baselines, maintaining comparable or superior J&F scores at substantially lower average refresh-call budgets and reducing false-positive failures. End-to-end runtime analysis further confirms that the overhead introduced by tracking, CLIP-based recurrence matching, and the identifiability gate remains modest relative to the dominant Sa2VA inference cost, thereby validating the efficiency of the proposed pipeline.

</details>


### [51] [Distill the Visual Evidence, Not Just the Answer: Cross-World On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38777)
*Yuanhao Sun,Huawei Ji,Jiaxin Ding,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨世界同策略蒸馏CW-OPD，显式监督学生对视觉证据变化的响应，并构建CWBench评测跨世界一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM蒸馏主要监督学生输出，视觉理解是隐式的；学生可能在不依赖相同视觉证据的情况下答对，因此需要确保学生真正响应决定答案的视觉信息。

Method: 为每个样本构造两个共享问题与场景、但答案关键证据不同的视觉世界，得到不同答案；在两个世界中执行同策略蒸馏，蒸馏教师的跨世界信念转移，使学生不仅匹配教师预测什么，还匹配预测为何随证据变化；并进行梯度分析。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B上，CW-OPD平均超过最强基线1.2个点；4B学生在CWBench上比DeepSeek-V4.1（552B）高22.4个CWPA点。

Conclusion: CW-OPD将依赖相关视觉证据变成显式蒸馏目标而非输出匹配的隐式结果，CWBench可用于诊断模型是否真正基于视觉证据作答。

Abstract: A central goal of vision-language model (VLM) distillation is to transfer both the teacher's language capabilities and its visual understanding. However, existing methods primarily supervise the student's output, leaving visual understanding implicit. Our analysis reveals that a student can match the teacher's answer without relying on the same visual evidence, raising the question: how can we ensure the student responds to the visual information that actually determines the answer? To this end, we propose \textbf{Cross-World On-Policy Distillation (CW-OPD)}, which explicitly supervises the student's response to changes in visual evidence. For each example, CW-OPD constructs two visual worlds that share the question and scene context but differ in answer-critical evidence, yielding different answers. We perform on-policy distillation in both worlds and distill the teacher's cross-world belief transition, encouraging the student to match not only \emph{what} the teacher predicts but also \emph{why} its prediction changes with the evidence. A gradient analysis shows that this term is invariant to errors shared by both worlds and supplies a corrective signal invisible to endpoint matching alone. In this way, CW-OPD makes reliance on the relevant visual evidence an explicit distillation target rather than an implicit consequence of output matching. To diagnose whether a model truly grounds its answers in visual evidence, we introduce CWBench, which measures cross-world consistency via Cross-World Pair Accuracy (CWPA). Experiments on Qwen3.5-4B show that CW-OPD outperforms the strongest baseline by \textbf{1.2} points on average, and the 4B student exceeds DeepSeek-V4.1 (552B) by \textbf{22.4} CWPA points on CWBench. Code is released in https://github.com/baokou-fw2/CWAD.

</details>


### [52] [CRAFT: Causal Responsibility and Failure Tracing in Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38810)
*Chunzheng Zhu,Jiaqi Zeng,Hongbo Zhao,Yihang Chen,Yijun Wang,Jianxin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究医学视觉语言模型中两种安全失效（文本覆盖视觉的仲裁失效、证据不足仍作答的刹车失效），发现其分别由空间上分离的注意力头群体介导，并提出CRAFT方法定位因果头集并验证其必要性/充分性，通过干预头集可在不重训练的情况下矫正失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在临床诊断中日益普及，但对其内部如何解决视觉与文本信号冲突缺乏理解；既有机制分析局限于单模态文本，无法解释为何一句误导性文本可覆盖正确的图像诊断，或模型在视觉证据不足时仍自信作答，这构成安全隐患。

Method: 提出的CRAFT方法通过双重标准将每种失效模式定位到最小因果注意力头集合，并借助时间探针（temporal probes）与Tuned Lens轨迹分析验证这些头的必要性（necessity）和充分性（sufficiency）。对仲裁头与刹车头分别进行消融干预，并观察其对冲突跟随与弃答行为的影响。

Result: 仲裁头构成中深层的宽频带，反映跨层证据竞争；刹车头集中于中后层窄频带，调控证据充分性与弃答行为。消融仲裁头可显著减少对冲突文本的跟随且对干净输入影响极小；消融刹车头可在视觉证据退化时恢复适当弃答。两类干预针对空间分离的头集，产生不同矫正效果。在多个医学VQA基准和多种VLM架构上验证了定位与干预的有效性，且无需重训练即可泛化。

Conclusion: 两类失效模式在机制上可分离，所识别的注意力头因果性地驱动各自失效模式，针对性调制可无需重训练地泛化，为VLM临床部署的安全性提供了机制层面的理解与干预手段。代码已公开。

Abstract: As vision language models are increasingly deployed in clinical diagnosis, understanding how they internally resolve competing visual and textual signals becomes a safety imperative. Existing mechanistic analyses remain confined to unimodal text and offer no explanation for why a single misleading sentence can override a correct image based diagnosis, or why a model commits to a confident answer despite insufficient visual evidence. We find that these two safety risks, arbitration failure where textual context overrides visual grounding and brake failure where the model commits without adequate evidence, are mediated by spatially disjoint attention head populations: arbitration heads form a mid-to-deep wideband reflecting cross-layer evidence competition, while brake heads concentrate in a narrow middle-to-late layer band that regulates evidence sufficiency and abstention behavior. To ground these observations in causal circuitry, we introduce CRAFT, which localizes each failure mode to a minimal causal head set via dual criteria and verifies necessity and sufficiency through temporal probes and Tuned Lens trajectory analysis. Excising arbitration heads sharply reduces conflict following with negligible degradation on clean inputs, while excising brake heads restores appropriate abstention under degraded visual evidence. The two interventions target spatially disjoint head sets and produce distinct corrective effects, underscoring the mechanistic separability of the failure modes. Experiments across multiple medical VQA benchmarks and VLM architectures validate both the localization and interventions, demonstrating that the identified heads causally drive each failure mode and that targeted modulation generalises without retraining. The code is available at GitHub repository.

</details>


### [53] [DCM-SAM: Defect-Conditioned Mixture of LoRA Experts for NPU-Deployed AM Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38811)
*Md Mushfiqur Rahaman,Md Mahedi Hasan,Imtiaz Ahmed,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCM-SAM：面向XCT金属增材制造缺陷检测的缺陷条件自适应LoRA专家混合方法，仅更新4.4%参数，在ViT-B上超越ViT-H基线，并首次揭示和解决了NPU端1024x1024分辨率下的激活内存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属增材制造零件的XCT检测面临三大现实约束：标注数据稀缺、关键气孔与夹杂物仅占几个像素、检测须在设备端（machine-side）完成。现有分割方法（如XCT-SAM）依赖大模型与真实标注，难以满足边缘部署需求。

Method: 提出DCM-SAM，一种缺陷条件自适应LoRA专家混合架构：以单个冻结的Segment Anything骨干为基础，为每类缺陷配置独立的Conv-LoRA专家库与掩码解码器，各类分别单独训练一遍，无需提示（prompt-free），仅用合成切片训练，可训练参数仅占4.4%。针对部署，进行数值等价的重写以支持NPU上的FP16推理。

Result: 在XCT-SAM报告的基准上，DCM-SAM以ViT-B骨干在两个缺陷类别上全面超越其ViT-H基线；在未见真实图像的条件下，于真实NIST扫描上达到64.2%气孔IoU。部署测试表明：在Qualcomm Hexagon NPU上，ViT-H与ViT-L虽可编译但无法在1024x1024分辨率下分配内存（瓶颈在激活而非权重，量化权重无效）；ViT-B原版可运行但适配后的编码器反而失败，经数值等价的注意力重写后，完整DCM-SAM可在FP16、1024x1024下运行，无算子回落CPU，掩码与FP32参考差异在0.01%像素以内。

Conclusion: DCM-SAM在参数高效、无监督提示、仅合成数据训练的条件下实现了跨域缺陷分割的SOTA表现，并揭示了边缘NPU部署中被忽视的激活内存瓶颈问题。通过数值等价的算子重写，证明了完整模型可在设备端FP16高分辨率下无损运行，为XCT检测的边缘化落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metal additive manufacturing parts are inspected by X-ray computed tomography, where labelled data is scarce, the pores and inclusions that matter span a few pixels, and inspection must happen at the machine. We present DCM-SAM, a defect-conditioned adaptive mixture of LoRA experts: one frozen Segment Anything backbone carries a separate Conv-LoRA expert bank and mask decoder per defect class, each trained in its own pass, without prompts, on synthetic slices alone, updating only 4.4% of the parameters. On benchmarks that XCT-SAM reports, DCM-SAM improves on every baseline for both classes from a ViT-B backbone against their ViT-H, and reaches 64.2% pore IoU on real NIST scans having seen no real images during training. Deployment then exposes what adaptation work rarely measures: on a Qualcomm Hexagon NPU, ViT-H and ViT-L compile yet cannot allocate at 1024x1024 image resolution, since activations rather than weights exceed the device ceiling, and quantizing weights does not help. ViT-B alone runs, but the adapted encoder then fails to allocate where the stock one succeeds, until a numerically identical rewrite of the attention lets the complete DCM-SAM run in FP16 at 1024x1024, with no operator falling back to the CPU, masks within 0.01% of pixels of the FP32 reference. Code: https://github.com/MushfiqShovon/DCM-SAM.

</details>


### [54] [Future Video Generation Better Aligns with the Human Visual Cortex than Observed Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38819)
*Chang-Bae Bang,Hyungjin Chung,Byung-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人类观看视频时视觉皮层fMRI响应与视频扩散模型内部表征的对齐关系，发现用于生成未来视频帧的表征比观察视频本身的表征更契合人脑预测性视觉加工，且这种对齐集中在高级视觉皮层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于视觉模型内部表征与人脑视觉皮层响应对齐的研究，大多忽略了人脑不仅加工当前观察到的视觉刺激，还会基于已有观察预测即将到来的刺激。因此作者提出假设：用于生成未来视频帧的内部表征，可能比观察视频本身的表征更契合人脑预测性视觉加工的本质。

Method: 研究比较了人类观看视频时的fMRI响应与两类视频扩散模型（自回归AR模型及其非AR基础模型）内部表征之间的对齐程度。首先在AR视频扩散模型内部进行对比分析，随后将AR模型与其非AR基础模型的内部表征进行比较，最后开展人类行为实验验证。

Result: 1）AR视频扩散模型中，用于未来视频生成的表征比观察视频的表征与视觉皮层对齐更好；2）与非AR基础模型相比，未来视频生成的表征同样比观察视频重建的表征对齐更好；3）观察视频重建的对齐主要集中在低级视觉皮层，而未来视频生成的对齐主要集中在高级视觉皮层；4）人类行为实验表明，人类更偏好通过放大与视觉皮层对齐更好的各层贡献所生成的视频。

Conclusion: 结果表明，面向未来视频生成的模型表征更能反映人脑视觉皮层的预测性加工机制，且这种对齐具有层级特异性（高级视觉皮层）。这为理解人脑视觉处理的预测本质以及构建更符合人脑加工的生成模型提供了新视角。

Abstract: Studying the alignment between the internal representations of vision models and the responses of the visual cortex to the same observed visual stimuli has enabled us to better understand human visual processing. However, studies so far have largely overlooked the fact that the human brain not only processes observed visual stimuli, but also predicts upcoming stimuli based on what has been observed. Accordingly, we hypothesize that internal representations for generating future video frames are better aligned with the predictive nature of human visual processing than representations of the observed video itself. To this end, we compare the alignment between human video-watching fMRI responses in the visual cortex and the internal representations from two types of video diffusion models, an autoregressive (AR) model and its non-AR base model. We first conduct a within-model analysis of the AR video diffusion model and show that the representations for future video generation align better with the visual cortex than the representations of the observed video. We then compare the internal representations of the AR model with those of its non-AR base model and again show that the representations for future video generation align better with the visual cortex than the representations for observed video reconstruction by the base model. Specifically, the alignment of observed video reconstruction is concentrated in lower-order visual cortex, whereas that of future video generation is concentrated in higher-order visual cortex. Finally, we show in a human behavioral experiment that humans prefer videos generated by amplifying the contributions of individual layers that align better with the visual cortex.

</details>


### [55] [DecoMoE: Decoupling Visual Propagation and Expert Computation for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38823)
*Xudong Tan,Peng Ye,Ming Xie,Chenyu Huang,Yaoxin Yang,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DecoMoE，从视觉传播与专家计算两个维度对多模态MoE模型进行结构化压缩，实现约1.69倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态MoE模型推理成本高：长视觉token序列反复触发注意力、路由、分发与专家MLP计算；现有方法通常只压缩token或专家单一维度，另一维度冗余仍存。

Method: 基于两个规律（视觉传播所需深度因输入而异；文本token路由呈现集中且循环的专家重要性），提出二维结构化压缩框架。包括Sample-Adaptive Visual Boundary (SAVB)，预测输入相关的视觉退出层并移除视觉token块；以及Routing-Calibrated Expert Prefix (RCEP)，离线按文本token路由质量重排专家，从预测退出层起每层保留覆盖目标路由质量比例的最短连续前缀。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-MoE与InternVL3.5-30B-A3B六个基准评估。Qwen3-VL-MoE上保留97.91%稠密基线性能，计算量由27.06降至16.73 TFLOPs，延迟由0.44降至0.26秒，获得1.69倍加速。

Conclusion: DecoMoE通过解耦视觉传播与专家计算，在两个维度同时压缩冗余，在几乎不损失性能的前提下显著降低多模态MoE推理开销。

Abstract: Multimodal mixture-of-experts (MoE) models combine sparse expert activation with visual-language capabilities, yet their inference remains costly because long visual-token sequences repeatedly incur attention, routing, dispatch, and expert-MLP computation. Existing methods typically compress either the token or expert dimension, leaving redundancy along the other. Our analysis reveals two complementary regularities: the depth required for visual propagation varies across inputs, while text-token routing exhibits concentrated and recurrent expert-importance patterns. Based on these observations, we propose DecoMoE, a two-dimensional structured compression framework that decouples visual propagation from expert computation. The Sample-Adaptive Visual Boundary (SAVB) predicts an input-dependent visual-exit layer at which the visual-token block is removed. The Routing-Calibrated Expert Prefix (RCEP) reorders experts offline using text-token routed mass and, from this predicted exit layer onward, retains at each MoE layer the shortest contiguous prefix covering a target routed-mass fraction. We evaluate DecoMoE on Qwen3-VL-MoE and InternVL3.5-30B-A3B across six benchmarks. On Qwen3-VL-MoE, DecoMoE retains 97.91% of dense-baseline performance while reducing computation from 27.06 to 16.73 TFLOPs and latency from 0.44 to 0.26 seconds, yielding a 1.69x speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/ShawnTan86/DecoMoE.

</details>


### [56] [FrameMorrow: Future-guided Frame Selection with Prospective Tokens for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38839)
*Bo Yin,Xiaobin Hu,Jiaqi Zhao,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FrameMorrow，一种前瞻式帧选择器，通过预测代表未来信息需求的前瞻token来指导历史帧选择，实现跨多种生成器的即插即用长视频生成增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成中历史记录随生成增长而变得昂贵且冗余，需要有效选择历史信息。但现有方法基于当前内容判断历史相关性存在缺陷：与当前相关的信息未必对未来有用，而看似无关的历史可能后续变得重要。因此历史信息的选择应依据其与未来信息需求的相关性。

Method: 核心洞察是无需生成完整未来，只需紧凑地表示"接下来什么变重要"即可指导历史选择。据此提出FrameMorrow前瞻式帧选择器：预测一小组前瞻token以表示未来信息需求，并用其从历史中识别相关信息。该方法选择显式历史帧而非模型特定的内部状态，从而支持跨多种生成器的即插即用集成，包括闭源模型，且推理开销极小。

Result: 在五个基准和11个生成模型上评估，涵盖长视频生成、交互式生成和动作条件世界模型。大量实验表明，在长程一致性、视觉质量和动作对齐方面均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: FrameMorrow通过面向未来信息需求的选择机制，为长视频生成提供了一种通用、高效且低成本的历史选择方案，在多样化生成设置中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation requires models to effectively leverage an increasingly long generation history. As the generated history grows, retaining all previous content becomes increasingly expensive and redundant, making effective historical selection essential. Existing approaches often determine historical relevance based on the current content. However, information relevant to the present is not necessarily useful for future generation, while seemingly less relevant history may become important later. Our key insight is that historical information should be selected according to its relevance to future information needs. Capturing these needs does not require generating the full future; instead, a compact representation of what becomes important next is sufficient to guide historical selection. Building on this insight, we propose FrameMorrow, a prospective frame selector that predicts a small set of prospective tokens representing future information needs and uses them to identify relevant information from history. FrameMorrow selects explicit historical frames rather than model-specific internal states, enabling plug-and-play integration across diverse generators, including closed-source models, with little additional inference cost. We evaluate FrameMorrow across five benchmarks and 11 generative models spanning long-video generation, interactive generation, and action-conditioned world models. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in long-range consistency, visual quality, and action alignment across diverse generation settings.

</details>


### [57] [Decoupling Spherical Reasoning from Dense Prediction for 360 Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38856)
*Zhijie Shen,Chunyu Lin,Shuai Zheng,Feng Li,Runmin Cong,Huihui Bai,Yao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出斐波那契球面图（FSG）与球面上下文调制（SCC）模块，将全景深度估计的上下文建模解耦到球面空间，在三个基准上取得更优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERP投影在全景深度估计中广泛使用，但其空间变化的畸变导致几何一致的特征建模困难，高度拉伸区域被过度表示。

Method: 1) 构建斐波那契球面图作为中间推理空间，将ERP特征提升到准均匀的斐波那契节点上；2) 通过互补的球面邻域捕获局部与长程依赖，节点近似均匀分布以减少拉伸区域过表示，且节点数紧凑、避免全分辨率建图的计算开销；3) 提出球面上下文调制（SCC）模块，用增强的球面表示自适应调制密集ERP特征，引导像素对齐的深度预测。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在深度精度上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将球面空间上下文建模与密集ERP预测解耦，是提升全景深度估计几何一致性与精度的有效途径。

Abstract: The equirectangular projection (ERP) is widely used for panoramic depth estimation, but its spatially varying distortion makes geometry-consistent feature modeling challenging. We revisit panoramic depth estimation by decoupling contextual modeling in native spherical space from dense ERP prediction. To this end, we propose a Fibonacci Spherical Graph (FSG) as an intermediate reasoning space to lift ERP features onto quasi-uniform Fibonacci nodes on the sphere and capture local and long-range dependencies through complementary spherical neighborhoods. The resulting spherical discretization distributes graph nodes approximately uniformly over the spherical surface, reducing the over-representation of highly stretched regions during relational modeling. Operating on a compact set of Fibonacci nodes also avoids the computational burden of constructing and processing a graph at full ERP resolution. To bridge spherical reasoning and dense prediction, we propose a Spherical Context Conditioning (SCC) module that adaptively modulates dense ERP features with the enhanced spherical representation, allowing spherical context to guide pixel-aligned depth prediction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves superior depth accuracy over existing approaches.

</details>


### [58] [AdaOcc: Adaptive 3D Occupancy Prediction for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38864)
*Jinglong Wang,Yunjie Wang,Zhiyang Zhang,Jiawei He,Ye Yuan,Bo Qiu,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaOcc，一种面向具身场景的基于点的自适应3D占据预测方法，通过自适应几何引导双分支编码器和渐进式查询学习，支持异构传感器与可调计算预算，在Occ-ScanNet上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身任务需要准确、灵活且语义丰富的3D场景表示。3D语义占据能建模整体3D空间，但现有占据预测方法难以满足实际部署需求，例如适应不同计算预算、传感器配置和观测视角。

Method: 提出基于点的自适应3D占据预测方法AdaOcc。采用自适应几何引导双分支编码器，支持不同视图数量的RGB图像（含估计深度）或LiDAR扫描；用稀疏语义点表示占据区域，并通过渐进式查询学习策略训练，使预测计算预算可通过查询点数量和解码器层数灵活调整；提出包含损失（containment loss）约束预测点落在有效占据区域内，以提升轻量点式占据学习的几何建模保真度。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在Occ-ScanNet上达到新的state-of-the-art，相较此前方法有显著性能提升；并且在真实具身系统中作为自适应3D感知模块展现出较强的实用适用性。

Conclusion: AdaOcc通过点式自适应设计同时兼顾异构传感器输入、可调计算预算与高保真几何建模，验证了其在具身场景中作为自适应3D感知模块的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Embodied tasks demand accurate, flexible, and semantically rich 3D scene representations. 3D semantic occupancy is well suited to this requirement, as it can model holistic 3D spaces by encoding geometric occupancy along with semantic categories. However, existing occupancy prediction methods struggle to meet practical deployment requirements, such as adapting to varying computing budgets, sensor setups, and observation views. In this paper, we propose a point-based Adaptive 3D Occupancy Prediction method, called AdaOcc, tailored for embodied scenarios. To accommodate heterogeneous sensor inputs, AdaOcc uses an adaptive geometry-guided dual-branch encoder that can support RGB images in various numbers of views with (estimated) depth maps or LiDAR scans. AdaOcc represents occupied regions via sparse semantic points trained with a progressive query learning strategy, allowing the prediction computational budget to be flexibly adjusted through query point numbers and decoder layers. To facilitate high-fidelity geometric modeling for lightweight point-based occupancy learning, we further propose a novel containment loss that regularizes predicted points to reside within valid occupied regions. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves a new state-of-the-art on Occ-ScanNet with considerable performance improvements over previous methods. Moreover, our framework demonstrates strong practical applicability as an adaptive 3D perception module in real-world embodied systems.

</details>


### [59] [MEMO: Multi-Level Entity-Aware Memory for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38900)
*Yinying Li,Yuqian Fu,Yulin Dai,Jingyu Gong,Tianwen Qian,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MEMO框架，通过多层级、实体感知的结构化记忆建模流式视频，无需训练即可即插即用，在StreamingBench和OVO-Bench上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需处理无界视觉流并在长时间跨度上保留丰富语义。现有方法主要关注扩大记忆容量（压缩为固定尺寸表示或扩展至GPU外存储），但依赖全局或粗粒度表示，不可避免地丢失细粒度视觉信息。

Method: MEMO通过多层级感知联合捕捉全局语义、实体动态和空间结构，将流式视频划分为语义连贯的块。每个块组织为结构化记忆：轻量级全局与实体级表示作为检索索引，对应高分辨率视觉内容单独留存以供按需访问。推理时进行查询特定的检索，选择性召回相关视觉证据用于下游推理。

Result: 在StreamingBench和OVO-Bench上，MEMO持续改进多个基础模型并取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 流式视频记忆应显式编码结构化且语义有意义的表示（尤其是实体层级）。MEMO无需训练、即插即用，验证了实体感知结构化记忆的有效性。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to process unbounded visual streams while preserving rich visual semantics across vast temporal horizons, posing a fundamental challenge for memory modeling. Existing approaches primarily focus on increasing memory capacity, either by compressing historical information into fixed-size representations or by extending storage beyond GPU memory. However, these methods largely rely on global or coarse-grained representations, inevitably losing fine-grained visual information. In this work, we argue that streaming video memory should explicitly encode structured and semantically meaningful representations, particularly at the entity level. To this end, we propose MEMO, a novel framework that models streaming video through multi-level, entity-aware structured memory. MEMO performs multi-level perception to jointly capture global semantics, entity dynamics, and spatial structures, partitioning streaming video into semantically coherent chunks. Each chunk is organized into a structured memory, where lightweight global and entity-level representations serve as retrieval indices, while the corresponding high-resolution visual content is retained separately for on-demand access. At inference time, MEMO performs query-specific retrieval over the structured memory and selectively recalls relevant visual evidence for downstream reasoning. Notably, MEMO is training-free and plug-and-play with existing multimodal large language models. Extensive experiments on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench demonstrate that MEMO consistently improves multiple base models and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [60] [FLOW: Feature-Level Optimal Warping for Generalized Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38913)
*Bo Zhao,Junzhe Cao,Dan Guo,Dongmin Huang,Wenjin Wang,Tao Tan,Yue Sun,Zitong YU*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出FLOW（特征级最优对齐）框架，利用最优传输实现域泛化的非接触式rPPG生理测量，通过时间细化模块与原型跨时间最优传输模块实现域不变对齐，并在四个基准上取得最优跨域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程光电容积描记（rPPG）可实现非接触式生理测量，但易受光照、运动和传感器等引起的域偏移影响，导致跨域泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出FLOW框架：集成时间细化模块（TRM）稳定时间动态，采用基于原型的跨时间最优传输（PCOT）模块通过可学习原型实现域不变对齐；使用软跨时间对应建模以保留生理信号固有节律；模块轻量，可无缝嵌入现有端到端rPPG架构；引入两个正则项保证源一致性与身份保持；并在条件最优传输下推导泛化界。

Result: 在四个rPPG基准上的大量实验表明，FLOW以轻量设计和强生理保真度实现了当前最优的跨域性能。

Conclusion: FLOW通过最优传输驱动的特征级时间对齐，有效缓解域偏移问题，在保持轻量化的同时提升rPPG跨域泛化与生理信号保真度。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact physiological measurement but remains vulnerable to domain shifts from illumination, motion, and sensors. We propose \textbf{FLOW (Feature-Level Optimal Warping)}, an \emph{optimal transport--driven} framework for domain-generalized rPPG. FLOW integrates a \textbf{Temporal Refinement Module (TRM)} to stabilize temporal dynamics and a \textbf{Prototype-based Cross-Temporal Optimal Transport (PCOT)} module to achieve domain-invariant alignment via learnable prototypes.Beyond feature alignment, FLOW employs soft cross-temporal correspondence modeling that aligns temporal features in a flexible manner, allowing the model to respect and preserve the intrinsic rhythmic patterns of physiological signals. Moreover, the lightweight design of our modules allows seamless integration into existing end-to-end rPPG architectures without additional preprocessing. Two regularization terms further enforce source consistency and identity preservation. Theoretically, we derive a generalization bound under conditional optimal transport. Extensive experiments across four rPPG benchmarks show that FLOW achieves state-of-the-art cross-domain performance with lightweight design and strong physiological fidelity.

</details>


### [61] [From Image Interpretation to Clinical Reasoning: Upstream Physician-Context-Aware Multimodal Learning with Causal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38924)
*Jialu Pi,Yanan Ma,Weijie Chen,Owen Crystal,Shubham Trivedi,Stephen Xie,Anna Silverman,Matthew Stib,Chadi Ayoub,Reza Arsanjani,Imon Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果强化学习框架，融合胸部X光片与临床病史进行多模态推理，用于机会性MACE风险预测，在内部、急诊及外部MIMIC队列上AUROC分别达0.720、0.760、0.845，并提升推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要不良心血管事件（MACE）是全球主要死因。利用常规采集的临床数据进行机会性筛查，可在急性事件发生前识别高风险个体，具备可扩展性。胸部X光片蕴含潜在心血管生物标志物，临床病史提供互补的患者背景，但现有医学视觉-语言模型主要面向放射学解读而非预后推理，因此需要一个能进行预后推理的多模态框架。

Method: 提出面向多模态临床推理的因果强化学习框架，整合CXR与医生撰写的临床病史进行机会性MACE预测，包含三项设计：（1）角色解耦的双LLM架构，将推理与风险预测分离；（2）双动作因果强化学习策略，用于证据选择与推理优化；（3）因果token剪枝，学习紧凑的多模态表示。

Result: 在内部队列、急诊科队列和外部MIMIC数据集上评估，该框架持续优于单模态基线和最先进的医学视觉-语言模型，AUROC分别为0.720、0.760和0.845；同时显著提升推理质量，获得更高的GREEN分数和更高的专家偏好，并在多样化患者群体中保持稳健的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该因果强化学习框架能够有效融合CXR与临床病史进行机会性MACE预测，兼具优异的预测性能与推理质量，并在不同患者群体中展现稳健性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE) remain the leading cause of mortality worldwide. Opportunistic screening using routinely acquired clinical data offers a scalable approach for identifying high-risk individuals before acute events occur. Although chest X-rays (CXRs) capture latent cardiovascular biomarkers and clinical histories provide complementary patient context, existing medical vision-language models are primarily optimized for radiology interpretation rather than prognostic reasoning. We propose a causal reinforcement learning framework for multimodal clinical reasoning that integrates CXRs and physician-authored clinical histories for opportunistic MACE prediction. The framework introduces (1) a role-decoupled dual-LLM architecture that separates reasoning from risk prediction, (2) a dual-action causal reinforcement learning policy for evidence selection and reasoning optimization, and (3) causal token pruning to learn compact multimodal representations. Evaluated on an internal cohort, an emergency department cohort, and the external MIMIC dataset, the proposed framework consistently outperformed unimodal baselines and state-of-the-art medical vision-language models, achieving AUROCs of 0.720, 0.760, and 0.845, respectively. It also substantially improved reasoning quality, achieving higher GREEN scores and higher expert preference while maintaining robust predictive performance across diverse patient populations.

</details>


### [62] [On the Relaxation of Conditional Independence Assumption for Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38930)
*Zixun Wang,Ben Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对RankSEG依赖条件独立假设(CIA)而忽略标签相关性的问题，提出空间局部依赖(SLD)结构替代CIA，并结合互易矩近似与不动点优化，将复杂度降至O(d log d)，在分割基准上持续超越argmax与CIA版RankSEG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RankSEG在推理时直接优化Dice/IoU，无需改动训练，但依赖限制性强的条件独立假设(CIA)，忽略了关键标签相关性，导致在模糊或低对比度场景下性能下降；而完整建模标签依赖计算代价高昂，需要O(d^3)时间。

Method: 用空间局部依赖(SLD)结构替代CIA，以可处理的方式捕捉局部标签相关性；再通过互易矩近似(Reciprocal Moment Approximation)配合新颖的不动点优化策略，消除穷举搜索，使算法复杂度降至实用的O(d log d)。

Result: 算法达到O(d log d)复杂度，并在多种分割基准上持续优于传统argmax和基于CIA的RankSEG；在低对比度或小目标场景中提升尤为显著，说明标签依赖能提供互补于图像信息的有效信号。

Conclusion: 通过引入局部标签依赖并优化计算，该方法在保持高效的同时提升了推理阶段的分割性能，验证了建模标签相关性对精确分割的价值。

Abstract: In semantic segmentation, a recent line of RankSEG methods directly optimizes Dice/IoU scores at inference time, improving alignment with evaluation metrics without modifying model training. Despite its theoretical and empirical success, RankSEG relies on the restrictive Conditional Independence Assumption (CIA), which ignores crucial label correlations and therefore degrades performance in ambiguous or low-contrast scenarios. However, accounting for full label dependence is computationally prohibitive, requiring $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ time. To address this, we replace the CIA with a Spatially Localized Dependence (SLD) structure that captures local label correlations while keeping the dependence model tractable. We further overcome the remaining computational bottleneck via a Reciprocal Moment Approximation coupled with a novel fixed-point optimization strategy that eliminates exhaustive search. The proposed algorithm achieves a highly practical $\mathcal{O}(d \log d)$ complexity and consistently outperforms conventional argmax and CIA-based RankSEG across diverse segmentation benchmarks. Improvements are significant in low-contrast or small-object scenarios, where label dependence offers valuable signals complementary to image information for accurate segmentation. The code of experiments is available at https://github.com/ZixunWang/RankSEG-DEP.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Spatial-Domain Supervision: A Relation Constrained Space for Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38968)
*Zeyu Wang,Mingyu Ge,Haiyu Song,Haoran Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出关系约束监督范式（RCS-Fusion），将多模态图像融合的监督从空间域转移到可学习的关系空间，利用冻结的DINO与CLIP模型提供信息并通过特征适配器推断共享性、主导性与协调半径三个关系参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMIF由于缺乏真实融合图像这一标准答案，通常以空间域源图像或梯度变体作为替代监督目标，导致监督机制与融合目标本身不一致，产生像素级折中或模态偏置问题。

Method: 1）构建可学习特征适配器，将异构的DINO和CLIP特征对齐到统一的监督空间；2）适配器推断共享性、主导性和协调半径三个关系参数，并据此定义三个与MMIF目标对应的损失；3）设计面向适配器的自监督对比排序目标以增强关系空间可靠性，并通过交替优化与融合网络耦合。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提监督空间在不同主流融合骨干网络上均可带来显著性能提升，说明其监督方式更契合MMIF目标。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种更符合MMIF目标的关系约束监督范式，通过冻结预训练表征模型与可学习适配器的结合实现关系空间的可靠监督，可广泛适配不同融合网络，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion (MMIF) aims to form a single image by integrating shared information, preserving complementary cues, and coordinating cross-modal conflicts across modalities. However, due to the absence of ground-truth fused images, existing MMIF supervision commonly uses spatial-domain sources or gradient variants as surrogate ground truth, making the supervision mechanism inherently misaligned with the goal of MMIF and causing pixel-level compromise or modality bias. To address this, we propose a relation-constrained supervision paradigm that moves fusion supervision from the spatial domain to a learned relation space. Rather than relying solely on direct source approximation, we further leverage frozen pretrained representation models as information providers and design a learnable feature adapter to align heterogeneous DINO and CLIP features into a unified supervision space. The adapter infers three relation parameters, namely sharedness, dominance, and coordination radius, which define three losses corresponding to the MMIF's goal. To make this space reliable, we devise a self-supervised contrastive ranking objective tailored to the adapter and couple it with the fusion network through alternating optimization. Extensive experiments show that the proposed supervision space yields significant gains regardless of which mainstream backbone the fusion network adopts, offering a supervision paradigm better aligned with the goal of MMIF. Code: github.com/GMY628/RCS-Fusion.

</details>


### [64] [PARK: Accurate Block Retrieval for Sparse Attention in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38978)
*Yun Dai,Jiarui Wen,Huiping Zhuang,Cen Chen,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARK是一种免训练的稀疏注意力方法，通过改进查询块与键块的检索聚合和聚类方式，提升Diffusion Transformers视频生成的块检索精度，在保持生成质量的同时加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers已成为视频生成的主流架构，但全注意力的二次复杂度限制了效率。稀疏注意力通过检索重要块来降低成本，然而不准确的检索会导致生成质量下降或产生不必要的计算。本文识别出基于查询块和键块平均表示进行检索时存在的两种不匹配问题。

Method: PARK是一种免训练的稀疏注意力方法。针对查询侧聚合不匹配，PARK保留每个原始查询，对其在键块上的注意力独立归一化，再在查询块内平均这些分布；针对键侧聚类度量不匹配，PARK利用当前查询信息对键进行变换后再聚类，使获得相似QK分数的键归为一组。此外还设计了一个融合GPU内核以降低块检索开销。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo和Wan上的实验表明，PARK提升了块检索精度，在加速推理的同时保持了生成质量，在所比较的稀疏注意力方法中取得了最佳的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: PARK通过解决稀疏注意力中查询侧聚合与键侧聚类度量的不匹配问题，实现了更准确的块检索，是一种有效且高效、无需训练的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become a dominant architecture for video generation, but their efficiency is limited by the quadratic complexity of full attention. Sparse attention reduces this cost by retrieving important blocks and computing attention only within them, but inaccurate retrieval can either degrade generation quality or yield unnecessary computation. We identify two retrieval mismatches in methods that retrieve blocks using the averaged representations of query and key blocks: (i) query-side aggregation mismatch, where averaging queries before Softmax fails to preserve their individual attention preferences, and (ii) key-side clustering metric mismatch, where standard Euclidean clustering in the original key space can group keys with dissimilar QK scores under the current query, so their average representation may not accurately represent how the current query scores individual keys. These mismatches can lead to inaccurate block retrieval. To address these mismatches, we propose PARK, a training-free sparse attention method for accurate block retrieval. PARK retains every original query, independently normalizes its attention over key blocks, and then averages these distributions within each query block. It also uses information from the current queries to transform keys before clustering, so that keys receiving similar QK scores are grouped together. A fused GPU kernel further reduces the overhead of block retrieval. Experiments on HunyuanVideo and Wan demonstrate that PARK improves block retrieval accuracy and preserves generation quality while accelerating inference, achieving the best quality-efficiency trade-off among the compared sparse attention methods.

</details>


### [65] [Mitigating Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models via False Discovery Controlled Visual Data Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38979)
*Chang Liu,Yu Tian,Rui Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CORAL，一个无需训练的框架，通过不确定性感知的视觉数据划分策略和镜像统计量来量化解码过程中的视觉对比，从图像层面控制多目标幻觉的假发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）存在多目标幻觉问题，即生成视觉输入不支持的物体，其根源在于解码过程中的视觉不确定性。现有方法虽使用对比信号减少幻觉，但依赖启发式规则，缺乏对图像层面假阳性的原则性控制。

Method: CORAL采用不确定性感知的视觉数据划分策略建模视觉不确定性，并利用镜像统计量在解码时量化视觉对比。通过对对称扰动的成对视觉输入计算镜像统计量，估算虚假物体预测，并设定数据驱动的阈值，以控制每张图像的期望假发现比例。

Result: 在多个基准测试和多种评估指标上的大量实验表明，CORAL持续优于现有最先进方法，提供更可靠、更稳健的幻觉控制。

Conclusion: CORAL是一个灵活的、无需重训或监督的框架，支持多种LVLM，能够在抑制幻觉的同时保留对真实接地物体的高检测能力。

Abstract: Multiple object hallucination, where large vision-language models (LVLMs) generate objects not supported by the visual input, is a persistent challenge caused by visual uncertainty during decoding. Existing methods reduce hallucinations using contrastive signals, but they rely on heuristics and lack principled control of false positives at the image level. To address this, we propose False Discovery Rate-COntRol of HALlucination (CORAL), a training-free framework that models visual uncertainty using an uncertainty-aware visual data splitting strategy and leverages mirror statistics to quantify visual contrast during decoding. By computing mirror statistics from paired, symmetrically perturbed visual inputs, CORAL estimates spurious object predictions and sets a data-driven threshold to control the expected fraction of false discoveries per image, suppressing hallucinations while retaining high power for truly grounded objects. The framework is flexible, supports multiple LVLMs, and mitigates hallucinations without retraining or supervision. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks with several evaluation metrics demonstrate that CORAL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, providing more reliable and robust hallucination control. Code is available at: https://changliu1993-cl.github.io/CORAL/

</details>


### [66] [MeshOctave generates meshes via cascading resolution transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38985)
*Junkai Lin,Tianhao Zhao,Hang Long,Huipeng Guo,Jielei Zhang,Youjia Zhang,Jiale Xu,Wenbing Li,Rendong Liang,Jozef Hladký,Matthias Nießner,Yuanming Hu,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MeshOctave，用二元空间网格分辨率定义尺度，通过确定性的顶点合并与"分割-重连"逆操作实现并行、动态长度的艺术家风格网格生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成紧凑、艺术家风格且具显式拓扑的网格方法存在不足：自回归模型逐 token 顺序生成代价高昂；连续流模型依赖启发式连通性解码器。next-scale 范式虽有潜力（并行同尺度预测、由粗到细细化），但现有方法通过渐进式网格简化构造层级尺度并需顺序逆转，导致同尺度并行性丧失、生成步数随面数线性增长。

Method: MeshOctave 用二元空间网格分辨率定义尺度：粗化被视为确定性坍塌，即合并共享同一体素单元的顶点并继承连通性；其逆操作"分割-重连"(split-and-rewire) 对每个粗面确定实例化哪些八分体子顶点，并用离散结构 token 解析局部连通性。这些逐面操作无需序列化，每次尺度转换建模为无序集合，每次增加一位坐标精度，天然支持动态长度网格与自适应分辨率细化。构建尺度条件化的掩码均匀离散扩散模型从分辨率坍塌层级中学习该操作。

Result: MeshOctave 在几何保真度和拓扑有效性上以显著优势超越强基线，同时支持自适应分辨率细化，并可自然扩展到网格细分任务。

Conclusion: 通过将尺度定义为空间分辨率并设计可并行、无序的尺度转换操作，MeshOctave 克服了自回归与流模型在网格生成中的局限，为艺术家风格网格生成提供了高效且支持自适应细化的新范式。

Abstract: Generating compact, artist-style meshes with explicit topology typically relies on autoregressive models which incur prohibitive sequential per-token costs, or continuous flow models that depend on heuristic connectivity decoders. Next-scale generation paradigms offer a compelling alternative by enabling parallel intra-scale token prediction and coarse-to-fine refinement from global structure to local topology; yet, existing methods derive hierarchical scales via progressive mesh simplification and invert them sequentially. This eliminates intra-scale parallelism and scales generation steps linearly with face count. In this paper, we propose MeshOctave, which instead defines scale through dyadic spatial grid resolutions, framing coarsening as a deterministic collapse that merges vertices sharing a voxel cell and inherits connectivity. Its inverse operation, split-and-rewire, determines which octant sub-vertices are instantiated for each coarse face and resolves local connectivity using discrete structural tokens. These per-face operations require no serialization, each scale transition is modeled as an unordered set that adds one bit of coordinate precision, naturally supporting dynamic-length meshes and adaptive resolution refinement. We construct a scale-conditioned masked-uniform discrete diffusion model to learn split-and-rewire operation from resolution collapse hierarchies. MeshOctave outperforms strong baselines in geometric fidelity and topological validity by a non-trivial margin, while supporting adaptive resolution refinement and extending naturally to mesh subdivision tasks.

</details>


### [67] [When Integral Meets Decomposition: A Signal-Level Self-Supervised Feature Decompose Paradigm for Multi-Modal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39004)
*Zeyu Wang,Jiayu Wang,Haiyu Song,Haoran Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIDFusion，将多模态图像融合中的特征分解从二维图像级监督重构为一维信号级积分驱动优化问题，采用两阶段自监督框架，在多个MMIF任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图像融合的特征分解范式将源图像分离为共同特征与模态特有特征，但缺乏GT分解特征图，只能组合多个图像级指标作为损失；这些损失本身不完整且可能相互冲突，因为每个像素耦合了纹理、边缘、轮廓等多种属性，导致监督不清晰。

Method: 提出一维信号级自监督特征分解范式，将特征分解从模糊的二维图像级监督重构为积分驱动的1D信号级优化问题，以共同信号与原始信号之间的积分面积来优化分解器，使优化目标明确且更稳定。模型采用两阶段SSL框架：阶段一设计双重预文本任务，即信号级积分驱动分解与图像级结构保持重建；阶段二融合特有特征并与共同特征结合以重建融合图像。

Result: 在代表性多模态图像融合任务上的实验表明该方法取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过目标层面的重构，将特征分解转化为具有清晰优化目标的一维信号级积分约束问题，为MMIF提供了一种更稳定有效的自监督分解范式。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion (MMIF) aims to integrate complementary information from different modalities into a high-quality fused image and support downstream tasks. Recently, feature decomposition has become an important paradigm by separating source images into common and modality-specific unique features. However, existing methods lack clear supervision because ground-truth (GT) decomposition feature maps are unavailable. They usually combine multiple image-level metrics as losses, which are inherently incomplete and may conflict since each pixel couples attributes such as texture, edge, and contour. To address this, we propose a 1D signal-level self-supervised feature decomposition paradigm. Our core insight is to reformulate feature decomposition from unclear 2D image-level supervision into an integral-driven 1D signal-level optimization problem. This objective-level reformulation uses the 1D signal form to compute the integral constraint. The decomposer is optimized by the integral area between common and original signals, enabling more stable optimization with a clear optimization objective. Our model follows a two-stage SSL framework. Stage I designs dual pretext tasks for integral-driven decomposition at the signal level and structure-preserving reconstruction at the image level. Stage II fuses unique features and combines them with common features to reconstruct the fused image. Experiments on representative MMIF tasks show state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance. Code: github.com/Wangjiayu0512/SIDFusion.

</details>


### [68] [Frame Differential On-Policy Self-Distillation for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39021)
*Haiying He,Xin Zheng,Shaoli Hu,Shijun Xiao,Xuanhe Liu,Bing Li,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FD-OPSD，将密集帧观测的证据在RL训练中通过token级自蒸馏迁移到稀疏帧策略，保持稀疏rollout与推理不变，在六个视频推理基准上优于GRPO、T-GRPO、Video-KTR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理中RL通常使用固定稀疏帧预算：帧数增加使自回归rollout昂贵，帧数过少又遗漏时间局部事件和细粒度视觉细节，需要在稀疏rollout下获取密集帧的证据。

Method: FD-OPSD比较同一采样响应在稀疏视图与密集视图下的token级偏好，提取"帧差异信号"并蒸馏到稀疏帧策略，无需外部教师模型，也无需密集自回归rollout。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B与Qwen3-VL-4B上、六个视频推理基准、16/32/64帧评测设置中，整体平均性能均高于对应的最强GRPO、T-GRPO、Video-KTR基线。

Conclusion: 密集视觉证据可在训练阶段通过token级自蒸馏被选择性迁移，同时保留稀疏帧rollout且推理过程不变。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of multimodal language models through verifiable rewards and increasingly fine-grainedvisual or temporal credit assignment. In video reasoning, however, current RL methods typically train with a fixed sparse frame budget: increasing the number of frames makes autoregressive rollouts expensive, while too few frames may miss temporally localized events and fine-grained visual details. We present \textbf{Frame Differential On-Policy Self-Distillation (FD-OPSD)}, which transfers the useful evidence of dense frame observations to a sparse frame policy during RL training. FD-OPSD compares the policy's token level preferences for the same sampled response under sparse and dense views, and distills the resulting frame differential signal without an external teacher or dense autoregressive rollout. The method preserves sparse-frame rollouts and leaves inference unchanged. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-4B on six video reasoning benchmarks, FD-OPSD yields higher overall average performance than the strongest corresponding GRPO, T-GRPO, or Video-KTR baselines across the 16, 32, and 64 frame evaluation settings. These results show that dense visual evidence can be transferred selectively during training through token level self-distillation while retaining sparse frame rollouts and unchanged inference.

</details>


### [69] [Persistent Watermarking of Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39024)
*Dixi Yao,Kaiwen Chen,Tahseen Rabbani,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对比式水印目标函数，通过显式鼓励加水印模型在触发输入上与原始模型行为不同，实现T2I模型版权保护，在多种下游修改下触发数据持久性显著优于现有方法，检测率常接近100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2I生成模型训练成本高昂，但其预训练模型可能被未授权获取并复用，攻击者可通过API提供修改版本。这些修改包括普通下游适配或蓄意抹除所有权（如输入提示预处理、模型微调、输出后处理）。核心挑战在于嵌入的触发数据需在这些变化下保持持久，同时不损害模型正常图像生成能力。

Method: 提出一种对比式（contrastive-style）水印目标函数，其中包含一个显式项，鼓励加水印模型在触发输入上的行为与原始模型不同，从而增强触发数据的持久性。

Result: 实验表明，在广泛的下游修改和蓄意削弱水印的尝试下，触发数据持久性显著强于先前方法，检测率更高，常接近100% TPR@FPR<10^{-4}。

Conclusion: 对比式水印目标能有效提升T2I模型水印在多种修改下的鲁棒性与检测率，为昂贵训练模型的版权保护提供可靠机制。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation is gaining increasing popularity with the general public, motivating the development of reliable mechanisms for copyrighting such models given their expensive training costs. An adversary may obtain and reuse a pretrained T2I model without authorization, and then serve a modified version through an API service. Such modifications may arise from ordinary downstream adaptation or deliberate attempts to erase ownership, including input-prompt preprocessing, model fine-tuning, and output post-processing. From the model owner's perspective, a key challenge is therefore to embed trigger data that remain persistent under such changes while preserving the model's normal image-generation capabilities. In this work, we propose a contrastive-style watermarking objective with a term that explicitly encourages the watermarked model to behave differently from the original model on trigger inputs. Experiments show substantially stronger trigger-data persistence than prior methods across a wide range of downstream modifications and deliberate attempts to weaken the watermark, resulting in higher detection rates, often approaching 100% TPR@FPR<$10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [70] [TED:Text-Axis Evidence Decomposition for Prompted Anomaly Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39033)
*JinYoung Kim,Geonho Kim,GiJeong Park,Geonu Lee,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TED（Text-Axis Evidence Decomposition）是一种无需训练的事后评分方法，通过在文本轴上分解证据，区分真实缺陷与"硬正常"竞争区域，从而提升CLIP类异常检测器的像素级定位可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP并非为细粒度缺陷定位设计，现有CLIP异常检测器通过prompt或轻量模块提升缺陷敏感性，但在域偏移下，增强的敏感性并不可靠：模型会对真实缺陷和视觉复杂的正常区域同时给出高异常分数。问题的根源不是缺乏缺陷信息，而是局部评分规则把"缺陷证据"与"硬正常证据"解码为相同的异常证据。

Method: 提出TED，一种事后（post-hoc）评分方法。它判断每个模糊响应更适合由源域缺陷patch支持，还是由被误判为异常的源域正常patch支持；在宿主模型的"正常vs异常"文本响应下比较这两种支持度；不改动主干和prompt，也不需要目标域训练。TED可作为原始VLM主干的免训练评分，或作为适配CLIP-AD宿主模型的源域校准残差修正。

Result: 在冻结的VLM主干上，TED相比原始prompt相似度显著提升像素级定位；在适配宿主上，在多数像素级设置（P-AUROC、P-PRO、P-AP）上均有提升。在硬假阳性竞争更强的场景增益最大，平均定位增益从低竞争情形的+5.0提升到中/高竞争情形的约+10.9。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预训练多模态表示中已存在可恢复的缺陷证据，但可靠的定位需要针对"硬正常"竞争者进行解码。代码将在TED GitHub仓库发布。

Abstract: CLIP is a powerful vision-language model, but it was not designed for fine-grained defect localization; CLIP-based anomaly detectors therefore adapt it with prompts or lightweight modules to increase defect sensitivity. We show that stronger sensitivity does not necessarily make local evidence reliable: under domain shift, adapted CLIP-AD models often assign high anomaly scores to both true defects and visually complex normal regions. The issue is not simply missing defect information, but a local scoring rule that decodes defect and hard-normal evidence, having the same anomaly evidence. We propose TED (Text-Axis Evidence Decomposition), a post-hoc scoring method that asks whether each ambiguous response is better supported by source defect patches or by source normal patches mistaken as anomalous. TED compares these supports under the host's normal-versus-anomaly text response, leaves the backbone and prompts unchanged, and requires no target-domain training. It works as a train-free score for raw VLM backbones or as a source-calibrated residual correction for adapted CLIP-AD hosts. Across frozen VLM backbones, TED substantially improves pixel-level localization over raw prompt similarity; across adapted hosts, it improves most pixel-level settings over P-AUROC, P-PRO, and P-AP. Gains are largest under stronger hard-FP competition, with mean localization gain increasing from +5.0 in low-competition regimes to about +10.9 in mid/high-competition regimes. These results suggest that recoverable defect evidence can already exist in pretrained multimodal representations, but reliable localization requires decoding it against hard-normal competitors. Code will be released at TED GitHub repository.

</details>


### [71] [BadAction: Backdoor Attacks on Interactive Video Generation via Action-Guided Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39047)
*Zhihang Wu,Zhongqi Wang,Jie Zhang,Fengming Gu,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究交互式视频生成（IVG）模型的后门攻击，提出 BadAction 方法，利用动作引导触发器植入后门，使模型在触发后生成冻结的未来帧，平均攻击成功率达 91.0%（仅动作触发器）和 80.4%（多模态触发器），并可绕过现有防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IVG 模型在可控视觉内容生成方面进展显著，但其安全漏洞（尤其是交互性方面的后门威胁）尚未被系统探索。作者希望填补这一空白，揭示 IVG 流程中的关键安全缺口。
