We find that pretrained language representations and conventional global behavior-language alignment capture coarse task semantics but often blur nearby specifications that require distinct behaviors. We introduce T3DP, a Text-to-3D Policy framework for fine-grained language-behavior alignment. Rather than compressing each instruction and demonstration into a single global embedding, T3DP preserves their local structures and establishes bidirectional token-level correspondence between linguistic elements and behavioral segments. This directly grounds subtle linguistic variations in the behavior components they affect, preventing closely related specifications from collapsing in the representation space. The resulting specification-sensitive language representation conditions a point-cloud-based 3D diffusion policy, enabling more precise control over unseen behavioral specifications without modifying the underlying policy architecture. Across Meta-World, ManiSkill, and RoboTwin, T3DP improves average held-out-specification success over global language-behavior alignment by +11.0-14.2 points, with gains on all 15 task families; on real-robot tasks, it further raises average success from 47.5% to 65.0% (+17.5 points). Representation and action-probe analyses show that fine-grained alignment better preserves specification geometry and action-relevant variation, linking local behavior grounding to downstream control.

</details>


### [278] [Towards Agile Vision-Based Multi-UAV Flight: Revisiting State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39611)
*Michal Pliska,Matouš Vrba,Ondřej Víta,Martin Jiroušek,Viktor Walter,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 集成倾角测量的位姿感知估计可消除仅位置测量带来的约300 ms结构延迟，使速度、加速度估计误差降低40%和57%，并在闭环NMPC跟随中实现超过2g的机动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷多无人机飞行需要机载实时、低延迟地估计邻居无人机运动状态以用于避碰与运动协调。多数视觉方法仅依赖位置测量，通过位移间接推断速度与加速度，这会带来固有结构延迟，限制可达敏捷性。

Method: 将先进视觉检测器提供的倾角测量（反映共面多旋翼推力方向）引入估计；对比4种仅位置估计器与5种位姿感知估计器，其中包括一种新的线性推力约束卡尔曼滤波（KF）公式；在2个真实数据集与1个高保真逼真仿真数据集上，按3–21 m/s²不同敏捷度评测。

Result: 位姿感知估计在三个数据集上平均将速度与加速度估计误差分别降低40%和57%，所提KF公式优于其他估计器；仅位置滤波在加速度阶跃响应中存在恒定约300 ms延迟且与敏捷度无关，而倾角约束估计器接近相机帧率给出的物理响应极限，因为它在位移累积前就观测到推力方向变化。

Conclusion: 在NMPC控制的闭环领航-跟随仿真中，仅位置估计无法使跟随者稳定悬停，而所提估计器可跟踪横向加速度超过2g的机动。

Abstract: Agile multi-UAV flight requires accurate and low-latency onboard estimation of the kinematic states of neighboring UAVs for collision avoidance, motion coordination, etc. Most vision-based approaches rely on position-only measurements, inferring velocity and acceleration indirectly from displacement. We show that this introduces a fixed structural delay in the estimation of higher-order states, which limits the achievable agility. To address this, we propose to integrate tilt measurements, provided by a state-of-the-art visual detector, which inform about the thrust direction of co-planar multirotor UAVs. We benchmark four position-only and five pose-aware estimators, including a novel formulation of a linear thrust-constraining Kalman filter, on two real-world and one high-fidelity photorealistic simulated dataset over different levels of agility (3-21 m/s^2). In our setup, pose-aware estimation consistently reduces the average velocity and acceleration estimation errors by 40% and 57% across the three datasets with the proposed KF formulation outperforming the other estimators. Position-only filters exhibit a constant ~300 ms delay in acceleration step response independent of agility, whereas the tilt-constrained estimators operate near the physical response limit given by the camera frame-rate by observing the change in thrust direction before the displacement accumulates. In a closed-loop leader-follower simulated experiment with NMPC control, position-only estimation of the leader's state fails to facilitate stable hovering of the follower, while the proposed estimator enables tracking of lateral maneuvers exceeding 2g of acceleration.

</details>


### [279] [TO-mdiSPAs: Topology Optimization of multi-directional Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39618)
*Swagatam Islam Sarkar,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于拓扑优化的系统方法，用于设计多方向软体气动执行器（SPA），兼顾制造鲁棒性与气动载荷的设计依赖性，获得了高性能非传统几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方向或全向SPA能在三维空间中任意方向弯曲运动，具有多自由度和丰富机械功能，但传统设计方法难以获得高性能、非常规的几何构型，因此需要系统性的优化设计方法。

Method: 采用拓扑优化（TO）方法；引入考虑blueprint、dilated、eroded三种设计的三场TO公式以保证制造鲁棒性；使用带排水项的达西定律建模气动载荷的设计依赖性；基于SPA单元目标输出变形构建min-max优化问题，并用移动渐近线法（MMA）求解。

Result: 优化得到一种高性能、非常规的几何设计；数值仿真表明所优化的SPA成功实现了多方向运动，具备良好的多功能性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套系统化的多方向SPA拓扑优化设计框架，验证了其在实现多功能多方向运动与制造鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators (SPAs) are highly promising and have been extensively explored within the field of soft robotics. Under pneumatic pressure loads, an SPA undergoes bending deformation to perform specific tasks. A multi- or omni-directional SPA can bend and move in any direction within a 3D space, leveraging its multiple degrees of freedom to realize various mechanical functions. This work presents a systematic methodology using topology optimization (TO) to achieve an optimized design for a multi-directional SPAs. To ensure manufacturing robustness, a three-field TO formulation considering blueprint, dilated, and eroded designs is implemented. Additionally, Darcy's law, incorporating a drainage term, is used to model the design-dependent nature of the pneumatic loading. A min-max optimization problem is formulated based on the target output deformations of the SPA unit and solved using the Method of Moving Asymptotes. The optimization yields a high-performing, unconventional geometric design. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate that the optimized SPA successfully achieves versatile multi-directional movements.

</details>


### [280] [Prediction is Better than Detection: Traffic Congestion Control using Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39637)
*Samira Hayat,Christian Raffelsberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机集群规模对交通拥堵检测与预测性能的影响，发现在车队规模接近监测路口数时性能趋于饱和，且基于预测的拥堵调控比基于检测的调控效果约翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：面向持续监测的移动机器人集群，其任务性能如何随规模扩展？当感知结果驱动下游动作（而非仅观察）时，这种扩展规律是否仍然成立？作者以无人机做交通拥堵检测与预测、并闭环驱动自适应信号控制为场景来回答该问题。

Method: 构建多智能体仿真：车辆遵循Nagel-Schreckenberg元胞自动机动力学，无人机采用轮询策略巡逻路口；对车队规模、交通水平和路网规模进行扫描，评估检测率、检测延迟和预测率。

Result: 性能在车队规模约等于被监测路口数时出现平台期；据此给出持续监测部署的通用车队配置规则。更关键的是，基于预测拥堵而非已检测拥堵来调整信号，可使拥堵时长的缩减幅度约翻倍，说明在"感知—行动"链路中，机载预测的价值可能超过增加机器人数量。

Conclusion: 预测精度而非感知覆盖度成为进一步提升的瓶颈，未来更有前景的方向是机载推理而非扩大车队规模。

Abstract: A central question in deploying teams of mobile robots for persistent monitoring is how task performance scales with fleet size, and whether this scaling holds once sensing drives downstream action rather than mere observation. We study this question for a team of drones performing traffic-jam detection and prediction in a simulated road network, whose reports drive an adaptive traffic-signal controller in closed loop. We build a multi-agent simulation, with vehicles following Nagel-Schreckenberg cellular-automaton dynamics and drones patrolling junctions via a round-robin policy, and sweep fleet size, traffic level, and network size to evaluate detection rate, detection delay, and prediction rate. We show how performance plateaus for fleet size approximating the number of junctions being monitored, and offer a general fleet-provisioning rule for persistent-monitoring deployments. More significantly, adapting the signal on a predicted jam, rather than a detected one, roughly doubles the resulting reduction in jam duration, showing that the value of onboard prediction in a sensing-to-action pipeline can exceed the value of adding more robots. Prediction accuracy, not sensing coverage, is now the binding constraint on further improvement, pointing to onboard inference, not fleet size, as the more promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [281] [Making Waves: A Membrane-Coupled Delta Array for Manipulating Objects Below the Actuator Spacing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39652)
*Bailey Dacre,Andrés Faíña,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过将8×8三自由度delta机器人阵列的末端执行器用可拉伸织物耦合，把64个离散接触点变成连续表面，从而打破执行器间距对可操作物体尺寸的下限限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式机械手系统依靠多执行器协同运动操纵物体，但物体必须同时被多个执行器支撑，因此执行器中心间距对可操作物体尺寸形成了硬性下限。作者希望消除这一尺寸限制。

Method: 将8×8个三自由度delta机器人阵列的末端执行器与可拉伸织物耦合，把64个离散接触转变为连续表面；把阵列视为表面上的位移场，研究局部准静态场与循环场作为操纵基元；这些基元可全局施加或局部限定在每个被跟踪物体周围以并行操纵多个物体；随后训练一个作用于低阶离散余弦变换系数的策略。

Result: 在相同动作维度下，命令一个19个delta的邻域相比命令整个阵列可将放置误差减半；策略无需自适应即可迁移到硬件，成功率为76%；平台可在单一表面上操纵15mm至90mm的物体，达到六倍尺寸范围，跨越执行器间距43.3mm的两侧。

Conclusion: 用可拉伸织物耦合离散执行器阵列可将离散接触转化为连续表面，突破执行器间距对物体尺寸的下限，并实现小至远低于间距、大至远超间距物体的单一表面操纵及多物体并行操作。

Abstract: Distributed manipulator systems manipulate objects through the coordinated motion of many actuators. However, an object must be supported by several actuators at once, so the centre-to-centre actuator spacing imposes a hard lower bound on manipulable object size. We remove this bound by coupling the end-effectors of an 8 x 8 array of three degrees-of-freedom delta robots with a stretchable fabric, turning 64 discrete contacts into a continuous surface capable of manipulating objects smaller than the actuator spacing. Viewing the array as a displacement field over that surface, we investigate local quasi-static and cyclic fields as manipulation primitives. These primitives can be applied globally across the array or locally confined around each tracked object to independently manipulate several objects in parallel. We then train a policy acting on low-order discrete cosine transform coefficients: at equal action dimension, commanding a nineteen-delta neighbourhood halves the placement error of commanding the whole array. The policy transfers to hardware without adaptation at 76% success. The platform manipulates objects from 15mm-90mm, a six-fold range spanning both sides of the actuator spacing, 43.3mm, on a single surface.

</details>


### [282] [From Local Whole-Body VLA Behaviors to Scene-Scale Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39670)
*Weixiang Guo,Rui Jin,Haotian Jin,Xinhang Xu,Ruiyang Liu,Haoran Zhao,Yi Wang,Weiqi Gai,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向关节式无人机操作臂（UAM）场景级空中操作的一体化框架，结合合成策略训练与 MPAR 执行对齐、场景图与拓扑迁移，在仿真与实物上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 VLA 模型扩展到场景级空中操作面临三大挑战：全身演示成本高、时延导致动作与状态错位、跨场地行为难以组合。

Method: 1) 场景可重构流水线：无需实物平台演示，合成任务条件、运动动力学可行的轨迹与多视角同步观测用于 VLA 训练；2) 测量进度对齐实现（MPAR）：将异步返回的动作块与实测执行进度对齐，并实现为连续、动力学可行轨迹；3) 关系场景图：将语言目标 grounding 到物体实例与可行交互区域；4) 拓扑引导迁移：跨场地连接局部行为。

Result: 局部 VLA 技能在 oracle 目标与可行交接条件下仿真成功率 39/60（65.0%）；在额外 500ms 时延（含/不含瞬时指令更新停滞）下，MPAR 相比标称时间对齐将接管阶段误差中位数降低 0.212s；完整系统完成 21/50 仿真多场地任务（42.0%），并在实物关节式 UAM 上进一步验证。

Conclusion: 该统一框架通过合成训练与进度对齐执行、场景图 grounding 及拓扑迁移，实现了场景级空中操作，缓解了演示成本、时延错位与跨场地组合问题，并具备仿真到实物的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable task-conditioned interaction, but extending them to scene-scale aerial manipulation remains challenging due to costly whole-body demonstrations, latency-induced action-state misalignment, and cross-site behavior composition. We present a unified framework for synthetic policy training and scene-scale execution on articulated uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs). A scene-reconfigurable pipeline synthesizes task-conditioned, kinodynamically feasible trajectories and synchronized multiview observations for VLA training without physical-platform demonstrations. Measured-progress-aligned realization (MPAR) aligns asynchronously returned action chunks with measured execution progress and realizes them as continuous, dynamically feasible trajectories. A relational Scene Graph grounds language goals to object instances and feasible interaction regions, while topology-guided transfer connects local behaviors across sites. Local VLA skills achieve 39/60 successes (65.0%) in simulation under oracle target and feasible-handoff conditions. Under 500-ms added latency, with and without a transient command-update stall, MPAR reduces median takeover phase error by 0.212 s over nominal-time alignment. The complete system completes 21/50 simulated multi-site missions (42.0%) and is further validated on a physical articulated UAM.

</details>


### [283] [RoboCoach: World Models as Active Coaches for Compositional Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39685)
*Jiajun Liu,Yifan Chen,Yichao Liu,Jiayi Zhang,Ruoqu Chen,Shaoxuan Xie,Guocai Yao,Mengdi Xu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ROBOCOACH世界模型引导的"教练"框架，用想象失败定位薄弱子任务，实现低成本定向示教与模块化策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作依赖技能复用，但用额外端到端示教提升组合能力代价高昂。自改进系统需解决两个问题：下一步教什么，以及把监督施加在哪里。

Method: ROBOCOACH采用Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve（RIDI）循环：在共享的动作条件世界模型COACHWORLD中执行可复用技能专家，用进度裁判记录首个未完成子任务；汇总失败记录以决定采集哪些子任务示教、更新哪些专家适配器。

Result: 两套仿真与两个真机平台上，想象与真实部署成功率在22个任务-策略对上相关（rho=0.840）；相同数据预算与更新计划下优于基线。仅150条额外子任务示教，Franka成功率由13.3%升至75.0%，AgileX由40.0%升至83.8%；教练后专家迁移至4个未见组合平均成功率35.0%，而均匀采样的共享策略基线为0%。

Conclusion: 世界模型可作为主动教练，将想象失败转化为定向监督，实现模块化策略的高效改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation reuses skills across many task compositions, but improving these compositions with additional end-to-end demonstrations is costly. A practical self-improving system must decide both what to teach next and where to apply that supervision. We present ROBOCOACH, a world-model-guided coaching framework that uses imagined failures to guide demonstration requests and expert updates. Its Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve (RIDI) loop executes reusable skill experts inside COACHWORLD, our shared action-conditioned world model, and uses a progress judge to record the first subtask that fails to complete. Aggregated records select which subtask demonstrations to acquire and which expert adapters to update. Across two simulation suites and two real-robot platforms, imagined and deployed success correlate over 22 task-policy pairs (rho = 0.840). Controlled comparisons show that our coaching method outperforms matched baselines under matched data budgets and update schedules. With only 150 additional subtask demonstrations, success rises from 13.3% to 75.0% on Franka and from 40.0% to 83.8% on AgileX. The coached experts also transfer to four held-out compositions, achieving an average success of 35.0%, compared with 0% for a shared-policy baseline updated with uniformly acquired demonstrations. Together, these results show that world models can serve as active coaches, turning imagined failures into targeted supervision for modular policy improvement. Project Page: https://robocoach-ai.github.io/

</details>


### [284] [Beyond Policy Alignment: Closing the Planning-Learning Loop for Robot Control with Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39751)
*Kowndinya Boyalakuntla,Yuhan Liu,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PL-MPC在TD-MPC的基础上改进critic监督与规划器终端价值估计，提升高维控制规划-学习闭环效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将学习到的世界模型与在线轨迹优化结合的高维控制中，规划器决定学习经验、而critic与actor又评估和生成未来计划，形成闭环。现有策略约束变体仅强化闭环的一部分，因此需要同时改进critic监督与规划器终端价值估计。

Method: PL-MPC在世界模型架构与MPPI优化器保持不变的前提下：(1)采用混合多步TD目标，使critic更新在自举前接触更多真实奖励；(2)使用分歧感知的终端估计降低MPPI规划中不确定critic值的影响；(3)通过回报加权actor蒸馏突出高回报回合中规划器执行的动作。

Result: HumanoidBench上最大提升出现于balance-hard（TAR从98±18升至387±255）和hurdle（从199±13升至466±200）；整体基准表现依赖任务，在DMControl上保持竞争力。消融显示两个任务中组件交互不同。在7-DoF KUKA IIWA14的wrench-nut对齐中实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，成功率高

Conclusion: PL-MPC通过同步改进critic监督与规划器终端价值估计增强规划-学习闭环，在多任务与sim-to-real中表现有效，但整体性能仍具任务依赖性。

Abstract: Planning with learned world models combines online trajectory optimization with learned value and policy functions for high-dimensional control. Because the planner determines the experience used for learning, while the learned critic and actor in turn score and propose future plans, planning and learning form a closed feedback loop. TD-MPC is a prominent instance of this design. Recent policy-constrained variants strengthen one part of the loop by aligning the learned policy with planner behavior. We introduce PL-MPC (Planning-Learning MPC), which additionally modifies critic supervision and planner terminal-value estimation. Hybrid multi-step TD targets expose critic updates to more realized rewards before bootstrapping; disagreement-aware terminal estimates reduce the influence of uncertain critic values during MPPI planning; and return-weighted actor distillation emphasizes planner-executed actions from high-return episodes. The world-model architecture and MPPI optimizer are otherwise unchanged. On HumanoidBench, the largest gains occur on \texttt{balance-hard}, where Total Average Return (TAR) increases from $98\pm18$ to $387\pm255$, and \texttt{hurdle}, from $199\pm13$ to $466\pm200$; performance across the broader benchmark remains task dependent, and PL-MPC remains competitive on DMControl. Controlled ablations show different component interactions across the two tasks. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wrench-nut alignment with a 7-DoF KUKA IIWA14, obtaining higher observed success than TD-M(PC)^2 on the training object size and two unseen sizes. Code and data will be available at: https://pl-mpc-humanoid.github.io.

</details>


### [285] [ChunkTrust: Adapting Execution Horizons for Robot Policies with Action-Expert Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39754)
*Fanding Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Shifeng Bao,Mingkang Pu,Shiwei Li,Jing Xu,Shijia Xu,Guanbo Huang,Chenghao Gu,Yuzhi Huang,Chenxin Li,Faisal Nadeem Khan,Huan Yang,Yan Wang,Cheng Chi,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChunkTrust 将机器人基础策略的动作执行步长（执行视界）视为一个从专家证据中推断的隐变量，而非固定超参，通过免训练的 AHS 与可选的 QHA 在多个仿真与真实任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础策略预测动作块，但重复规划前应执行多少动作取决于当前任务阶段，固定执行视界这一超参设置并不合理，因此需要动态、自适应的视界选择方法，且最好不依赖额外训练。

Method: 提出 ChunkTrust：其免训练的 Action-aware Horizon Selector (AHS) 结合块内生成轨迹的谱稳定性（intra-chunk spectral stability）与已执行历史同预测动作之间的块间连续性（inter-chunk continuity）两类证据；用带核遗忘的在线 Beta 后验在多次重规划中跟踪视界偏好；可选的轻量 Query-based Horizon Adapter (QHA) 从互补证据学习上下文条件的稠密先验，并与当前证据及回合局部 Beta 记忆融合，同时保持基础策略冻结。

Result: 在 RoboTwin2.0 和 RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop 上，AHS 对每个评测的基础策略配置均提升了任务平均成功率，如在 50 个 RoboTwin2.0 任务上使 π_{0.5} 提升 +6.80 个百分点，在 RoboCasa 上使 Qwen3GR00T 提升 +9.67 个百分点；AHS+QHA 在八任务 π_{0.5} 评测中把相对 Base 的增益提高到 +9.44 个百分点。在四个真实家庭任务上，AHS 将等任务平均归一化过程分数从 50.4% 提升至 57.5%。消融实验考察了两类证据项、时间记忆与学习先验的贡献。

Conclusion: 将执行视界建模为可从证据推断的隐变量是有效且通用的，AHS 在免训练下即能稳定提升多种基础策略的成功率与真实任务表现，可选 QHA 可进一步带来增益，且两类证据、时间记忆与学习先验均各有贡献。

Abstract: Robot foundation policies predict action chunks, but how many actions to execute before replanning depends on the current task phase. We introduce ChunkTrust, which treats the execution horizon as a latent variable inferred from action-expert evidence rather than a fixed hyperparameter. Its training-free Action-aware Horizon Selector (AHS) combines intra-chunk spectral stability of generation traces with inter-chunk continuity between executed history and predicted actions. An online Beta posterior with kernel forgetting tracks horizon preferences across replans. A lightweight Query-based Horizon Adapter (QHA) optionally learns a context-conditioned dense prior from complementary evidence, fused with current evidence and episode-local Beta memory while the base policy remains frozen. Across RoboTwin2.0 and RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop, AHS improves overall task-averaged success for each evaluated base-policy configuration, including gains of +6.80 percentage points on $π_{0.5}$ over all 50 RoboTwin2.0 tasks and +9.67 percentage points on Qwen3GR00T in RoboCasa. AHS+QHA raises the gain over Base to +9.44 percentage points on the eight-task $π_{0.5}$ evaluation. On four real-world household tasks, AHS improves the equal-task mean normalized process score from 50.4% to 57.5%. Ablations examine the contributions of both evidence terms, temporal memory, and the learned prior. Project page is https://hf618.github.io/ChunkTrust.github.io/

</details>


### [286] [Experience-Driven Continual Learning of Terrain Traversability for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39755)
*Luca Bricarello,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Alberto Sanchez-Delgado,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种持续学习框架，用冻结DINOv3预接触图像特征预测5种本体感受交互指标及不确定性，经门控经验回放持续更新，并投影为保守可通行性地图用于四足导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在陌生地形上安全高效导航，需在接触前预判地形-机器人交互结果（滑移、足端载荷、能耗等），而仅靠几何与视觉外观无法揭示这些交互特性；同时部署环境不断变化，需要模型持续适应且不遗忘旧知识。

Method: 使用冻结的DINOv3骨干生成预接触描述子，映射到5个按测量可靠性加权的本体感受指标（平面足滑移、平均法向地面反作用力、牵引指数、运输成本、触地加载率）；采用紧凑证据回归器同时预测指标及偶然/认知不确定性；持续学习结合有界经验回放与验证门控（仅当新模型在近期留出数据上提升、且在历史留出数据上退化在容差内时才替换）；预测与认知不确定性投影到局部多层地图，合成权重可调而无需重训的保守可通行性评分图，用于下游导航。ROS2实现在Unitree Go2仿真与硬件上分别训练评估。

Result: 在三种未见地形的顺序硬件数据流上，门控回放相比无门控回放将最终锚点NLL退化降低23.1%，同时新地形适应性能相当。

Conclusion: 验证了以经验驱动的预接触交互结果预测加门控持续学习，可有效缓解灾难性遗忘并支撑四足机器人跨陌生地形的安全导航。

Abstract: Safe and efficient quadruped navigation over unfamiliar terrain requires predicting terrain-robot interaction before contact: geometry and visual appearance alone cannot reveal how the robot will slip, load its feet, or expend energy. This paper presents a continual learning pipeline that uses locomotion experience to learn these interaction outcomes from pre-contact images and continually updates the predictions as new contacts are observed. Pre-contact descriptors, produced by a DINOv3 backbone model frozen during training, are mapped to five proprioceptive indicators weighted according to measurement reliability: planar foot slip, mean normal ground-reaction force, traction index, cost of transport, and touchdown loading rate. A compact evidential regressor allows us to predict these indicators together with aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty from the visual descriptors. Continual adaptation combines bounded experience replay with a validation gate: candidate models replace the deployed predictor only when they improve performance on recent held-out data while keeping degradation on historical held-out data within a prescribed tolerance. Predictions and epistemic uncertainty are projected into a local multilayer map and combined into a conservative traversability score map whose property weights can be adjusted without retraining. The resulting map is used for downstream navigation tests. The ROS2 implementation supports evaluation on a Unitree Go2 in simulation and on hardware, with models trained separately in each domain. On a sequential hardware stream over three previously unseen terrains, gated replay reduces final anchor negative log-likelihood (NLL) degradation by 23.1% relative to replay without the gate while attaining similar new-terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [287] [DiffWAM: A Fast and Efficient Navigation World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39763)
*Mo Zhu,Yuze Wu,Xijie Huang,Xiao Cui,Fei Gao,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffWAM 是一种几何条件化的导航世界-动作模型，直接从冻结视频模型的预测表征中恢复运动，将多层预测特征转化为连续相机轨迹，避免部署时的未来视频解码与多帧重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频基础模型蕴含丰富的语义与时空先验，但将其转化为无人机运动通常需要昂贵的未来视频合成与几何重建。作者希望探究是否可以直接从冻结视频模型的预测表征中恢复未来视觉预测中的隐含运动。

Method: 提出 DiffWAM：几何条件化的导航世界-动作模型，直接将多层预测特征变换为连续相机轨迹。Grid-Motion 模块保持时空运动关联，Latent2Pose 用首帧几何进行落地以恢复具有度量意义的三维运动。完整视频推演与几何重建仅用于离线监督，部署时免除未来视频解码与多帧重建。另提出 FastDreamer，将预测与几何计算与飞行过程重叠，并进行时间戳感知的异步轨迹交接，实现无人机连续执行。

Result: 在 1000 样本的 DiffWAM-1000 基准上，轨迹 RMSE 为 0.3492 m，终点成功率 74.40%。真实世界实验展示了受限穿越、环绕、S 形飞行与多阶段导航等复杂行为。机载 DiffWAM-Flash 在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 上模型流水线延迟为 1.08 s。

Conclusion: 结果表明预测性视频表征可被高效地落地为连续三维运动，为“先生成再重建”的导航流水线提供了一种直接替代方案。

Abstract: Pretrained video foundation models encode rich semantic and spatiotemporal priors for embodied navigation, yet converting these priors into UAV motion typically requires expensive future-video synthesis and geometric reconstruction. We investigate whether the motion implicit in future visual prediction can instead be recovered directly from the predictive representations of a frozen video model. To this end, we present DiffWAM, a geometry-conditioned navigation world-action model that directly transforms multi-level predictive features into continuous camera trajectories. Its Grid-Motion module preserves spatial-temporal motion associations, while Latent2Pose grounds them with first-frame geometry to recover metrically meaningful 3D motion. Complete video rollouts and geometric reconstruction are required only for offline supervision, eliminating future-video decoding and multi-frame reconstruction during deployment. We further introduce FastDreamer, which overlaps predictive and geometric computation with ongoing flight and performs timestamp-aware asynchronous trajectory handoff for continuous UAV execution. DiffWAM achieves a trajectory RMSE of 0.3492 m and an endpoint success rate of 74.40% on the 1,000-sample DiffWAM-1000 benchmark, while representative real-world experiments demonstrate complex behaviors including constrained traversal, orbiting, S-shaped flight, and multi-stage navigation. An onboard DiffWAM-Flash implementation further reaches 1.08 s model-pipeline latency on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor. These results demonstrate that predictive video representations can be efficiently grounded into continuous 3D motion, providing a direct alternative to generate-then-reconstruct navigation pipelines. Project page: https://zzmmzzm.github.io/diffwam.github.io/.

</details>


### [288] [Tool-Policy Co-Design for Powder Weighing in Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39797)
*Nikola Radulov,Xin Yang,Kevin S. Luck,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出工具-策略协同设计框架，同时优化粉末分装工具形态与控制策略，通过双层优化与几何相似度热启动，使真实称量误差较标准工具降低45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自动化中自主粉末称量是瓶颈之一，源于异质材料的复杂非线性动力学。机器人化学家使用为人类手部灵巧性设计的标准工具，其固定几何形状决定了控制策略需要调节的动力学，限制了性能。

Method: 构建工具-策略协同设计框架，形式化为双层优化，在目标粉末流动性分布上最小化分装误差。外层用贝叶斯优化与hyperband调整工具深度、宽度及边缘尖刺拓扑等设计参数，内层为每个候选形态优化控制策略。还提出几何相似度度量，从结构相似设计的缓存策略热启动训练，同等算力下多探索28%的配置。在流动性感知的机器人-材料仿真框架中于7种不同物理动力学材料上评估。

Result: 协同设计的工具形态使真实世界称量误差相对标准工具降低45%，且在先前未见材料上同样有效。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时使控制策略与物理工具适应目标材料的动力学，为实验室自动化领域带来材料操作的新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous powder weighing is one of many bottlenecks in laboratory automation due to the complex, non-linear dynamics of heterogeneous materials. Robot chemists performing this task utilise standard tools shaped for the dexterity of human hands, whose fixed geometry sets the dynamics that the control policy needs to regulate. This work introduces a tool-policy co-design framework that concurrently optimises the morphology of a dispensing tool and its control policy for use by robots in chemistry laboratories, formulated as a bi-level optimisation that minimises dispensing error over a target distribution of powder flowabilities. The outer loop varies tool-design parameters such as tool depth, width and rim spike topology using Bayesian optimisation and hyperband, while an inner loop optimises a control policy for each candidate morphology. We also introduce a geometric similarity metric that warm-starts policy training from cached policies of structurally similar designs, exploring 28% more configurations under the same compute budget. The proposed framework is evaluated on a robotic powder weighing task across seven materials with distinct physical dynamics in a flowability-informed robot-material simulation framework. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-designed tool morphology reduces real-world weighing errors by 45% relative to a standard tool, including on previously unseen materials. These results demonstrate our method can adapt both the control policy and the physical tool to the dynamics of the target material, bringing a new paradigm for material manipulation to the field of laboratory automation.

</details>


### [289] [Learning from Runtime Feedback through Failure-Bank Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39820)
*Mingyue Cui,Zheyuan Liu,Yihan Zhu,Zheyuan Zhang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FailBank 四阶段自演化框架，将运行时安全反馈转化为持久的策略改进，在 VLA 操作任务中兼顾成功率与接触代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在复杂环境中需平衡任务成功与意外接触；运行时屏蔽（runtime shield）只能修正单步动作，不改变底层策略，反复分歧会造成策略与屏蔽的持续失配，阻碍任务推进。

Method: 四阶段自演化框架：采集阶段以固定的 CBF 安全模块作为仅观察教师，在策略仍掌控动作时生成反事实修正；随后采用结果感知的准入机制，将有用建议转为纠正目标，并把成功且无需修正的动作保留为“静默锚点”，用于受保护的 LoRA 更新。

Result: 在 VLA-Arena 基准（两个难度、两种 VLA 骨干）上：相比基座策略，成功率提升 8.5 与 6.9 个百分点，策略引发的累计代价降低 35.6% 与 23.8%；相比运行时屏蔽，成功率提升 25.4 与 9.5 个百分点，且累计代价相当。

Conclusion: 运行时反馈可作为持久的策略监督，而不仅是临时的动作约束，从而改善成功—代价联合操作点。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize broadly across robotic manipulation tasks, but complex environments require balancing task success with unintended contact. Runtime shields can correct individual actions, but they leave the underlying policy unchanged, so repeated disagreements may create a persistent policy-shield mismatch that blocks task progress. To address this challenge, we introduce FailBank, a four-stage self-evolving framework that converts runtime feedback into persistent policy improvement. During collection, a fixed CBF-based safety module serves as an observe-only teacher, producing counterfactual corrections while the policy remains in control. Outcome-aware admission then converts useful proposals into corrective targets and retains successful uncorrected actions as quiet anchors for guarded LoRA updates. We evaluate FailBank on the VLA-Arena benchmark across two difficulty levels and two VLA backbones. Compared with the base policies, FailBank improves the joint success-cost operating point. Across the two backbones, FailBank improves task success rate by 8.5 and 6.9 percentage points, while reducing policy-induced cumulative cost by 35.6\% and 23.8\%, respectively. Compared with runtime shielding, FailBank raises task success rate by 25.4 and 9.5 percentage points, while maintaining comparable policy-induced cumulative cost. These results show that runtime feedback can serve as persistent policy supervision rather than only as a temporary action constraint.

</details>


### [290] [Toward Real-Time VLAs: Stage-Aware Two-Step Flow Denoising and System-Level Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39822)
*Di Wu,Rongtian Shen,Ping Liu,Yan Shen,Zhenhan Yin,Shun Zuo,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Jianglin Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型低推理速率与高机器人执行速率的时序鸿沟，本文提出两阶段非均匀去噪与分布式实时VLA框架，将去噪步数从10降至2、推理时间从61.557 ms降至21.956 ms，并在服装折叠任务中评估六种实时执行方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型存在低速率推理与高速率机器人执行之间的时序鸿沟。反复的Flow Matching去噪显著增加推理开销，而机器人侧延迟主要来自感知采集、通信调度与物理响应，因此需要联合优化模型推理效率与机器人系统时序。

Method: 通过端到端测量刻画模型推理与机器人执行链的延迟；分析速度场发现早期积分阶段幅值与方向较稳定、末端步骤出现更强方向校正，据此提出两阶段非均匀去噪；并构建分布式实时VLA框架，支持推理、动作发布与机器人控制独立速率、模块化观测采集及动作溯源日志。

Result: 两阶段非均匀去噪将步数从10降至2，模型推理时间从61.557 ms降至21.956 ms。以π0.5为基线，在长时程物理服装折叠任务上评估六种实时执行方法：训练类方法中Legato整体最优，免训练方法中Temporal Smoothing最优，二者在任务成功率、完成时间、动作连续性与加速度平滑性上表现良好。两步去噪结合代表性执行方法可大幅降低推理开销，任务性能仅有小幅下降。

Conclusion: 结果支持对模型推理效率与机器人系统时序进行联合优化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models face a timing gap between low-rate inference and high-rate robot execution. We characterize this gap through end-to-end latency measurements of model inference and the robot execution chain. Repeated Flow Matching denoising contributes substantially to inference cost, while robot-side delays mainly arise from perception acquisition, communication scheduling, and physical response. Analysis of the velocity field shows relatively stable magnitude and direction in early integration, followed by stronger directional correction near the terminal steps. Based on this stage heterogeneity, we propose two-stage non-uniform denoising, reducing the number of steps from 10 to 2 and model-inference time from 61.557 ms to 21.956 ms. We also develop a distributed real-time VLA framework with independent inference, action-publication, and robot-control rates, modular observation acquisition, and action-provenance logging. Using π0.5 as the baseline, we evaluate six real-time execution methods on a long-horizon physical garment-folding task. Legato performs best overall among training-based methods, while Temporal Smoothing leads among training-free methods; both perform strongly in task success, completion time, action continuity, and acceleration smoothness. Combining two-step denoising with representative execution methods substantially reduces inference cost with a small reduction in task performance. These results motivate joint optimization of model-inference efficiency and robot-system timing.

</details>


### [291] [RL-Guided PAC-NMPC for Probabilistically-Safe Perception-Based Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39854)
*Adam Polevoy,Dillon Capalongo,Katherine Tang,Mark Gonzales,Marin Kobilarov,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将随机非线性模型预测控制（SNMPC）与强化学习（RL）相结合的方法，实现未知环境中基于感知的概率安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于感知的强化学习导航策略在未知环境中应用时安全性难以保证，缺乏概率意义上的安全约束，因此需要一种能在保持RL长时程性能的同时满足概率安全约束的方法。

Method: 首先用RL训练概率化的actor-critic模型和传感器预测模型；然后将这些概率模型嵌入基于采样的SNMPC框架（即PAC-NMPC），利用硬约束对碰撞概率和价值函数改进施加有限时间的统计保证，确保有限时程SNMPC策略在期望上降低价值函数。

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法能提升基于感知的RL导航策略的安全性，并可扩展到具有大传感器输入空间和复杂非线性动力学的高维系统；硬件实验在未知环境中用敏捷固定翼飞行器验证了视觉导航性能的改善。

Conclusion: 将RL的概率模型与PAC-NMPC的安全约束相结合，可在满足概率安全约束的同时逼近RL的长时程性能，并在仿真与真实硬件上验证了有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an approach for combining stochastic nonlinear model predictive control (SNMPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) to enable probabilistically-safe perception-based navigation in unknown environments. Our method first uses RL to train probabilistic actor-critic and sensor prediction models. We then leverage these probabilistic models in a sampling-based SNMPC framework known as Probably Approximately Correct (PAC)-NMPC, which uses hard constraints to enforce finite-time statistical guarantees on the probability of collision and value function improvement. By ensuring that our finite-horizon SNMPC policies decrease the value function in expectation, we can approach the long-horizon performance of the RL approach while satisfying probabilistic safety constraints. Through simulation experiments, we show that our approach can improve the safety of perception-based RL navigation policies and scale to high dimensional systems with large sensor input spaces and complex nonlinear dynamics. We also demonstrate our approach through hardware experiments, showing improved performance for vision-based navigation with an agile fixed-wing aerial vehicle in unknown environments.

</details>


### [292] [Magic-W0: A Structured World-Action Foundation Model for Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39870)
*Xuhua Chen,Zhenhan Yin,Yuan Zhang,Lingfeng Zhang,He Zheng,Tong Mu,Shun Zuo,Dian Zhou,Di Wu,Xuan Zhou,Shaojie Wan,Rongtian Shen,Qiulong Xu,Yiduo Li,Yinglong Wang,Yanqian Wang,Kun Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Magic-W0世界-动作基础模型，将机器人交互表示为结构化的"当前状态—过渡—未来状态"世界转移，并通过分层对齐的世界-动作交互架构耦合预测与控制，在仿真与真机任务上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）多依赖未来观测重建或通用潜表示预测，缺乏与动作生成紧密耦合的结构化、面向控制的世界表示，难以有效支撑机器人策略学习。

Method: Magic-W0将交互建模为结构化世界转移：当前状态融合视觉-语言上下文与当前3D几何，过渡由3D运动表示，未来状态由未来语义表示。提出分层对齐的世界-动作交互架构：演化中的动作假设为世界转移预测提供条件，预测的世界表示又反过来指导动作生成。基于大规模第一人称人类操作、UMI、真机及仿真数据预训练，并利用预训练视觉模型对几何、3D运动与未来语义施加潜监督。

Result: 推理时干预实验表明，结构化世界表示能随候选动作变化系统性响应，动作相关信息可经由共享的3D表示传播至未来语义预测；在RoboDojo-Sim上平均得分27.10，为对比WAMs中最高；多项真机任务经有限下游数据微调后表现良好。

Conclusion: 结构化世界表示与动作生成的双向耦合有助于提升世界-动作模型的预测一致性与控制性能，支持泛化与快速适应。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment robot policies with action-conditioned environment dynamics, yet existing approaches largely rely on future observation reconstruction or generic latent prediction and lack structured, control-oriented world representations tightly coupled with action generation. We introduce Magic-W0, a world-action foundation model that jointly models structured physical state evolution and continuous actions. Magic-W0 represents interaction as a Structured World Transition consisting of Current State, Transition, and Future State. Current State combines vision-language context with Current 3D Geometry; Transition is represented by 3D Motion capturing action-induced three-dimensional changes; and Future State is represented by Future Semantics describing task-relevant outcomes. To couple prediction and control, we propose a layer-aligned world-action interaction architecture in which evolving action hypotheses condition world-transition prediction, while predicted world representations continuously inform action generation. Magic-W0 is pre-trained on large-scale egocentric human manipulation, UMI, real-robot, and simulation data, with latent supervision for geometry, 3D motion, and future semantics from pre-trained visual models. Inference-time interventions show that structured world representations respond systematically to changes in candidate actions and that action-related information propagates through shared 3D representations into future semantic predictions. On RoboDojo-Sim, Magic-W0 achieves an average Score of 27.10, the highest among the compared WAMs. Across multiple real-robot tasks, it also demonstrates strong downstream performance after fine-tuning with limited downstream data, supporting generalization and rapid adaptation.

</details>


### [293] [SplineWAM: Adaptive Action Horizons for World Action Models via B-Spline Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39873)
*Jun Guo,Xiaoshen Han,Qiwei Li,Nan Sun,Peiyan Li,Heyun Wang,Hang Lai,Weinan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SplineWAM 提出用固定大小的三次 B 样条参数窗口自适应压缩动作轨迹，使动作块时长与分辨率随运动复杂度变化，并配合 JP-RTC 支持异步部署，在提升成功率的同时减少策略调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）每次推理输出固定长度的动作块，计算预算在时间上均匀分配。这导致自由空间运动无法执行更久、接触密集操作无法获得更多推理，限制了云端服务时的吞吐量。

Method: 1）将动作轨迹自适应压缩为固定尺寸的立方 B 样条参数窗口，节点时间根据运动特性拟合，同一参数预算可解码出不同时间分辨率与持续时长的动作块；执行跨度与下次策略调用间隔由预测本身决定。2）将视频监督对齐到示教轨迹的拟合节点时间而非均匀网格，使监督帧集中在动作复杂处。3）提出雅可比回拉实时分块（JP-RTC）用于异步部署，在解码后的原始动作上施加块连续性约束，并通过解码器校正参数，使已执行前缀与已提交动作一致。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboCasa 上，成功率分别比动作分块 WAM 提升 8.2 和 4.4 个百分点，同时每回合策略调用次数减少 22% 和 26%。在三个双臂真实机器人任务异步执行下，效果领先或持平基线，每次调用解码执行的运动量提升 1.2 至 1.6 倍。

Conclusion: SplineWAM 通过样条参数化动作表示与自适应的时序分配，突破了固定长度动作块的均匀时间预算限制，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低推理调用频率并提高执行吞吐，适用于云端异步部署场景。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are large embodied policies that jointly predict future video and the actions to execute, emitting a fixed-length action chunk per inference call. Such a policy allocates its computational budget uniformly in time, unable to execute for longer over free-space motion or to spend more inference on contact-rich manipulation, which limits the throughput a WAM can reach when served in the cloud. We present SplineWAM, which adaptively compresses the action trajectory into a fixed-size window of cubic B-spline parameters, fitting the knot times to the characteristics of the motion. One parameter budget then decodes into chunks of varying temporal resolution and duration, and both the executed span and the interval until the next policy call follow from the prediction itself. Aligning the video supervision to the fitted knot times of the demonstration rather than to a uniform grid concentrates the supervised frames where the action trajectory is complex. For asynchronous deployment we introduce Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking (JP-RTC), which imposes chunk continuity on the decoded raw actions the robot executes rather than on the spline parameters, and corrects the parameters through the decoder so that the executed prefix agrees with the actions already committed. On LIBERO-Plus and RoboCasa, SplineWAM improves success rate over an action chunking WAM by $8.2$ and $4.4$ points while cutting policy calls per episode by 22% and 26%. On three bimanual real-robot tasks under asynchronous execution, it leads or matches the baseline while decoding 1.2 to 1.6 times as much executed motion per call.

</details>


### [294] [Active Mapping of Underwater Litter Using Camera-Sonar Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39898)
*David Rete,Patrick Boros,Lucian Busoniu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动建图框架，融合前视声呐与相机到共享贝叶斯占据栅格图，通过每步求解优化问题选择下一最佳视角，实现高效水下垃圾搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋垃圾对水下生态系统威胁加剧，需要能在大范围高效定位碎片的自主动搜寻方法。现有方法多沿预设路径行进或仅使用单一传感模态——相机在低能见度下成像质量差，声呐则噪声大、分辨率低。

Method: 构建主动建图框架：前视声呐与相机共同输入共享的贝叶斯占据栅格图；每步求解优化问题决定下一最佳视角。候选视角由双项效用函数评分，权衡基于体素熵的不确定区域探索与对可能目标的利用。各传感器由数据确定的、随距离和方位变化的检测概率与虚警概率表来表征。

Result: 在逼真的水下模拟器中评估，主动建图比割草机式覆盖模式更快找到目标，且双传感器方案优于单独使用任一传感器。

Conclusion: 融合声呐与相机的主动建图方法能提升水下垃圾搜寻效率，双模态互补优于单一模态。

Abstract: Marine litter is a growing threat to the underwater ecosystem, driving demand for autonomous survey methods that can locate debris efficiently over large areas. Existing survey methods typically follow predefined paths or operate with a single sensing modality, typically a camera (with image quality suffering in poor-visibility conditions) or sonar (usually noisy and low-resolution). We present an active mapping framework in which a forward-looking sonar and a camera both feed into a shared Bayesian occupancy map, and an optimization problem is solved at each step to decide on the next best view. Candidate viewpoints are scored by a two-term utility that balances exploration of uncertain regions via voxel entropy against exploitation of likely objects. Each sensor is characterized by range- and bearing-dependent detection and false-alarm probability tables determined from data. We evaluate the approach in a realistic underwater simulator, demonstrating that active mapping finds objects faster than a lawnmower coverage pattern, and that the dual-sensor approach works better than using either of the individual sensors.

</details>


### [295] [Magnetic based In-situ Self 3D Pose Estimation for a Modular Soft Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot via IMU-Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39950)
*Zheng Cao,Guo Ning Sue,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Junzhe Hu,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种嵌入式位姿感知框架，融合IMU与主动磁场实现连续体机器人无外部视觉的实时构型估计与闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因其固有柔顺性和对复杂环境的适应能力而适合进行轻柔操作，但其连续可变形结构导致精确构型估计困难，尤其在外部视觉系统不可用或被遮挡时。

Method: 构建嵌入式位姿感知框架，结合惯性测量单元（IMU）与主动磁场；将IMU的角度测量与磁场参考进行融合，以改善局部姿态估计并减少运行中姿态误差的累积。该方案更新率达16.7 Hz，可支持实时反馈。

Result: 通过闭环控制实验验证，利用估计的机器人构型在末端执行器与物体交互时维持其于期望位置；实现了16.7 Hz的实时更新率。

Conclusion: 分布式磁-惯性感知在连续体机器人的实时位姿估计与闭环控制中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for gentle manipulation because of their inherent compliance and ability to adapt to complex environments. However, their continuously deformable structure makes accurate configuration estimation challenging, particularly when external vision systems are unavailable or obstructed. In this work, we present an embedded pose sensing framework that combines inertial measurement units (IMUs) and active magnetic fields to estimate the robot configuration without relying on external cameras. The angular measurements from the IMU and magnetic-field references are fused to improve local orientation estimation and reduce accumulated orientation error during operation. This pose sensing scheme achieves an update rate of 16.7~Hz, allowing real-time feedback. The proposed system is experimentally validated through closed-loop control, where the estimated robot configuration is used to maintain the end-effector at a desired position while interacting with an object. These results demonstrate the potential of distributed magnetic--inertial sensing for real-time pose estimation and closed-loop control of continuum robots.

</details>


### [296] [TACTIC: Temporal and Context-Aware LLM Tactical Planning for Roadside LiDAR Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39969)
*Yiming Gao,Shaocheng Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACTIC是一个利用多模态大语言模型进行场景自适应路侧LiDAR攻击的框架，通过语义场景图选择攻击策略，在CARLA实验中达到100%碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理LiDAR攻击评估多采用固定原语和手动参数，忽视了攻击效果对周围交通环境的强依赖性，导致难以发现场景相关的失效模式。

Method: 在灰盒威胁模型下，仅依赖攻击方自建的路侧感知栈。局部感知提供车辆度量状态，MLLM结合路侧图像推断交通关系上下文并构建语义场景图。基于此选择并配置两种互补原语：push-away（偏移前车感知距离）和phantom-obstacle braking（注入障碍触发急刹）。用实测交通状态与经验标定约束保证物理可行，并采用异步推理-执行重叠机制配合高速感知触发的重规划。

Result: 280次随机CARLA试验中，完整策略碰撞率达100%，对比固定规则35%、随机选择60%、受限LLM模式选择加默认参数75%。物理与图像联合输入成功率100%，仅物理65%，仅图像75%；异步Δ刷新将场景突变响应时间从7.4秒降至2.0秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，场景相关的战术规划能够暴露固定攻击策略可能忽略的、对上下文敏感的LiDAR失效模式。

Abstract: Physical LiDAR attacks are often evaluated using fixed primitives and manually selected parameters, despite their strong dependence on surrounding traffic. We present TACTIC, a scene-aware framework that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to coordinate state-adaptive roadside LiDAR attacks. Under a gray-box threat model, TACTIC relies only on an attacker-operated roadside perception stack, without accessing the victim LiDAR's native point clouds or internal processing. Local perception provides metric vehicle states, while the MLLM combines these measurements with roadside imagery to infer relational traffic context and construct a semantic scene graph. Based on this representation, TACTIC selects and configures two complementary primitives: \emph{push-away}, which shifts the perceived range of a lead vehicle, and \emph{phantom-obstacle braking}, which triggers emergency braking through obstacle injection. Measured traffic states and empirically calibrated constraints ground the generated tactics in physically feasible operating regions. To accommodate MLLM latency, TACTIC overlaps reasoning and execution asynchronously while high-rate local perception detects scene changes and triggers replanning. Across 280 randomized CARLA trials, the full policy achieves a 100% collision rate, compared with 35% for a fixed rule, 60% for random selection, and 75% for a restricted LLM using mode selection with default parameters. Joint physical-and-image input achieves 100% success, versus 65% with physical measurements alone and 75% with imagery alone, while asynchronous $Δ$ refresh reduces scene-mutation response from 7.4 s to 2.0 s. These results show that scene-dependent tactical planning can expose context-sensitive LiDAR failure modes that fixed attack policies may miss.

</details>


### [297] [PhasePlan: Ordered Future-Phase Planning for Robot Brain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39970)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Yafei Zhang,Wensheng Li,Qing Zhan,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种有序未来阶段规划方法，用于机器人脑模型的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人脑模型多预测固定长度的动作块，可能跨越多个任务阶段，从而模糊阶段转换并偏向高频动作模式，损害动态环境中的动作时序。

Method: 从当前多模态观测出发，预测未来每个动作位置对应的任务阶段；由此得到的规划表示用于条件化相应动作，保持任务进度与动作生成之间的时序对齐。训练时先学习规划器，随后在动作模型适配阶段冻结该规划器，以维持稳定的阶段表示。方法在预训练的π0.5和AcrossWAM1.0机器人脑模型上实例化。

Result: 在π0.5实现上的定量评估显示，在传送带操作任务中，相比原始π0.5模型，离线关节动作误差降低约22.5%；同时改善了阶段转换建模和跨阶段动作预测。

Conclusion: 结果表明有序未来阶段规划对连续动作生成具有价值。

Abstract: Robot brain models integrate vision, language, and robot state to generate actions for complex manipulation tasks. Most predict fixed-length action chunks that may span multiple task phases. This can obscure phase transitions and favor frequent action patterns, compromising action timing in dynamic environments. We propose \method, an ordered future-phase planning method for robot brain models. From current multimodal observations, it predicts the task phase at each future action position. The resulting planning representations condition the corresponding actions, preserving temporal alignment between task progress and action generation. Training first learns the planner, then freezes it during action-model adaptation to maintain stable phase representations. We instantiate \method on pretrained $π_{0.5}$ and AcrossWAM1.0 robot brain models. Detailed quantitative evaluation uses the $π_{0.5}$ implementation. On conveyor-belt manipulation, \method reduces offline joint-action error by approximately 22.5\% relative to the original $π_{0.5}$ model. It also improves phase-transition modeling and cross-phase action prediction. These results demonstrate the value of ordered future-phase planning for continuous action generation.

</details>


### [298] [When Instructions Retrieve Trajectories: Diagnosing and Mitigating Generalization Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39971)
*Hung-Jen Chen,Yu-Hsun Hou,Yan-Hong Chen,Yan-Fu Chen,Binghua Cai,Min Sun,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出VLA模型存在"指令-动作绑定"失败：策略同时接收语言和视觉输入，却未能将二者结合来选择任务所需动作；作者提出等变反事实训练（ECT），在LIBERO-PRO上将位置交换成功率从36%提升至59%，并在真实UR5e上把未见位置成功率从8%提升至88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在分布内任务上成功率可超90%，对不改变所需动作的干扰变化也有鲁棒性，但在需要改变动作的反事实变化下会失败。聚合鲁棒性分数掩盖了这一更具体的失败模式：策略对语言和视觉都有响应，却没有将两者结合以选择正确动作，即"指令-动作绑定"失败。作者希望揭示并解决这一问题。

Method: 作者先对微调的π_{0.5}和GR00T-N1.7策略进行行为分析，发现失败轨迹常保留源行为或切换到另一个已演示任务，说明语言并非被忽略；并通过读出与干预将选择关联到任务条件化的内部状态。对模仿目标的分析表明，狭窄的条件动作支撑会使"基于视觉接地"与"基于指令键控"的解法在演示上不可区分。据此提出ECT，在两个层面起作用：数据层面提供有效演示，使同一指令在可区分场景中需要不同动作；损失层面将每条演示与其对应配对样本在同一次更新中训练。

Result: 在受控的LIBERO-PRO对比中，完整ECT将π_{0.5}的平均位置交换成功率从36%提升到59%；在CALVIN上，由于配对样本已存在于原始数据中，ECT损失无需新演示即可提升五任务完成率；在固定演示预算下的真实UR5e上，完整ECT将未见位置成功率从8%提升到88%。

Conclusion: VLA模型的失败可归因于指令-动作绑定不足，而非单纯忽略语言。通过提供反事实配对演示并同步训练，ECT能有效改善模型在反事实变化下的动作选择，在仿真与真实机器人上均带来显著提升，且在有配对数据时无需新增演示。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can exceed 90% success on in-distribution tasks and withstand nuisance changes that preserve the required action, yet fail under counterfactual changes that demand a different action. Aggregate robustness scores can therefore conceal a more specific failure, in which a policy responds to both language and vision yet does not combine them to select the action the task requires. We call this failure instruction-action binding. Instructions cue familiar trajectory families, and visual feedback adjusts their execution. Behavioral analyses of fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.7 policies reveal that failed rollouts often retain the source behavior or switch to another demonstrated task. These switches show that language is not simply ignored. Readouts and interventions connect these choices to task-conditioned internal states. Our analysis of the imitation objective shows how narrow conditional action support can leave grounded and instruction-keyed solutions indistinguishable on the demonstrations. This motivates Equivariant Counterfactual Training (ECT), which acts at two levels. ECT data supply valid demonstrations in which the same instruction requires different actions in distinguishable scenes, while the ECT loss trains each demonstration with its counterpart in the same update. In a controlled LIBERO-PRO comparison, full ECT raises $π_{0.5}$'s mean position-swap success from 36% to 59%. On CALVIN, where counterparts already occur in the original data, the ECT loss improves five-task completion without new demonstrations. On a real UR5e under a fixed demonstration budget, full ECT raises unseen-position success from 8% to 88%.

</details>


### [299] [EWAM: Emergent Depth-Wise Specialization in a Unified Embodied Model -- From Semantic Understanding through Visual Foresight to Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39973)
*Hao Wang,Jiajun Wen,Jingzhi Liu,Shuoshuo Xue,Zhiliang Chen,Min Lin,Yicheng Chang,Xiaoyu Guo,Yukang Zhuo,Zheng Chong,Yunshuang Nie,Jian Zhang,Weijia Liufu,Qingman Wu,Heming Xu,Bingchang Song,Dantong Wu,Zhiyuan Wang,Hang Xu,Jianhua Han,Bokui Chen,Shen Zhao,Rui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EWAM，一个以动作为中心的统一具身模型，通过非对称联合注意力让动作token在每一层读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来与动作信息，并自发形成"浅层语义→中层预测→深层动作"的深度分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略侧重语义理解，WAM侧重环境动态预测表征，但即使系统同时暴露于两类信息，动作计算仍往往集中于单一专家，缺乏对两类信息的有效整合与分工。

Method: EWAM采用非对称联合注意力：动作token在每层可读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来帧和动作token信息，而感知专家保持各自独立角色。无需逐层监督，模型在两种预训练范式（跨具身机器人轨迹、人类第一视角视频）下训练，并引入子任务阶段监督。

Result: 模型自发涌现深度特化：浅层动作查询主要关注视觉-语言特征，中层关注预测未来帧，深层关注动作token自身；该交接跨任务可复现、跨去噪步稳定。检查点追踪与因果干预证明该分工是习得的且为动作生成所依赖。仿真与真机实验中EWAM超越现有VLA、WAM及混合基线；人类第一视角数据提升跨具身迁移与真机鲁棒性，子任务阶段监督提升长程任务完成度。

Conclusion: 统一具身学习能够诱导从语义理解、经视觉前瞻、到动作形成的有序内部递进过程。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies emphasize semantic understanding, whereas world-action models (WAMs) learn predictive representations of environment dynamics. Systems that expose a policy to both sources often still concentrate action computation on a single expert. We present EWAM, an action-centric unified embodied model whose asymmetric joint attention lets action tokens read semantic, current-visual, predicted-future, and action information at every layer while the perceptual experts retain their distinct roles. Without layer-wise supervision, EWAM develops an emergent depth-wise specialization: action queries attend mainly to vision-language features in shallow layers, to predicted future frames in intermediate layers, and to action tokens themselves in deep layers. This handoff replicates across tasks and is stable across denoising steps. Checkpoint tracking and causal interventions show that it is learned and that action generation depends on it. EWAM is pretrained in two separate regimes, one on cross-embodiment robot trajectories and one on human egocentric video. In simulation and real-robot experiments, it surpasses existing VLA, WAM, and hybrid baselines. Human egocentric data improve both cross-embodiment transfer and real-robot robustness, and subtask-phase supervision improves long-horizon completion. Together, these results suggest that unified embodied learning can induce an ordered internal progression from semantic understanding, through visual foresight, to action formation.

</details>


### [300] [Multi-Link Safety Filtering for VLA Policies Around Moving Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40007)
*Yatharth Agarwal,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的多连杆安全盾，将夹爪、腕、前臂用五个椭球包裹，在运行时过滤VLA指令，并借稀疏光流跟踪障碍，显著降低碰撞、提升安全成功率且满足边缘实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略即使任务成功，也可能在杂乱环境中撞倒无关物体，说明任务成功不等于部署安全；且需在不重训策略的前提下于运行时防护，并面临防护范围需覆盖整条机械臂、障碍会移动、需与策略共享板上算力等挑战。

Method: 免训练安全盾：用5个椭球覆盖夹爪、腕部和前臂，将每条指令动作通过单一障碍程序，与复位时由RGB-D感知拟合的禁入椭球进行比较过滤；再用稀疏光流随障碍移动携带该椭球中心，避免重复检测与重拟合；并对π0.5策略压缩视觉-语言前缀、减少流匹配步数以加速策略调用。

Result: 6种仿真危险运动条件下，碰撞率从65.62%降至27.27%，安全成功率从29.35%升至50.43%；消融显示防护机械臂连杆优于仅防护末端执行器，跟踪能挽回大部分冻结估计损失的防护。异构边缘硬件上，5椭球障碍程序CPU运行99分位2.2ms，π0.5单次调用在集成GPU上从343ms降至177.3ms；真实SO-101机械臂4个任务中，16个屏蔽回合仅3次触碰危险物，而未屏蔽为11次。

Conclusion: 无需重训即可在运行时为预训练VLA提供多连杆、可跟踪、低开销的安全防护，兼顾安全与实时性，并在真实机械臂上初步验证有效。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy can finish a manipulation task while knocking over objects unrelated to it, so task success alone does not show that the policy is safe to deploy in clutter. We study how to keep a pretrained VLA policy clear of such hazards at run time without retraining it, which requires guarding more of the arm than the end effector, following the hazard as it moves, and sharing onboard compute with the policy. Our training-free shield covers the gripper, wrist, and forearm with five ellipsoids and filters every commanded motion through one barrier program against a keep-out ellipsoid fitted from RGB-D perception at reset. Sparse optical flow then carries that ellipsoid's center along with the hazard, with no repeated detection or refitting. Over six simulated hazard-motion conditions, the shield lowers collision from $65.62\%$ to $27.27\%$ and raises safe-success, task completion without collision, from $29.35\%$ to $50.43\%$. Ablations show that guarding the arm links protects beyond end-effector shielding, and that tracking recovers most of the protection lost when the hazard estimate is frozen at reset. On heterogeneous edge hardware, the five-ellipsoid barrier runs on the CPU in $2.2$~ms at the 99th percentile, and trimming the vision--language prefix and taking fewer flow-matching steps shortens each $π_{0.5}$ policy call on the integrated GPU from $343$ to $177.3$~ms. On a physical SO-101 arm across four tasks, the arm touched the hazard in 3 of 16 shielded episodes versus 11 of 16 unshielded ones. Project page: https://yathag.github.io/multilink-safety-filter/

</details>


### [301] [Identifiable Decomposition of Submovements in Human Hand Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40012)
*Adrian Prados,James Hermus,Ramon Barber,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出以时空核相关性作为可辨识性准则，并据此设计 Sub-ID 分解方法，用于从运动数据中提取子运动基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有子运动分解方法扩展性差，且缺乏判断分解是否有信息量的原则性标准。

Method: 将基元对之间的时空核相关性作为可辨识性准则，嵌入自适应岭正则化中，使优化偏向低相关解，从而在基元共线时失去可辨识性、空间分离时恢复。

Result: 在合成数据上，Sub-ID 能恢复现有方法失败的参数分布；当基元重叠过重无法区分时，能显式检测该模糊性而非输出误导结果；并从真实三维长时运动数据中成功提取子运动。

Conclusion: Sub-ID 有望为运动控制研究与模仿学习识别具有生理依据的基元。

Abstract: Voluntary movements have long been hypothesised to be comprised of discrete primitives called submovements, as a descriptive model of human motor behaviour. However, existing methods scale poorly, and no principled method exists to determine whether a decomposition is informative. We propose a spatiotemporal kernel correlation between primitive pairs as an identifiability criterion. Submovement-Identifiable Decomposition (Sub-ID) embeds this criterion in its adaptive-ridge regularisation, biasing the optimiser toward low-correlation solutions. Identifiability is lost when primitives become collinear and recovered when they diverge spatially. On synthetic data, Sub-ID recovers ground-truth parameter distributions where existing methods fail; furthermore, when primitives overlap too heavily to be distinguished, the method explicitly detects this ambiguity rather than outputting misleading results. Sub-ID extracts submovements from real three-dimensional, long-horizon movements, a regime no prior method addresses. This method has the potential to identify physiologically grounded primitives for motor control research and imitation learning.

</details>


### [302] [BatSLAM 2.0: Sequence-Verified Sonar Place Recognition in a Robust Pose Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40085)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 BatSLAM 2.0，一种仅依赖声呐的 SLAM 系统，通过改进声学前端、序列验证器与位姿图解决声呐场所识别的歧义与错误回环导致的拓扑地图崩溃问题，并在仿真与真实环境中验证其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠利用回声定位在黑暗杂乱环境中导航。此前 BatSLAM 证明仿生双耳声呐机器人可通过识别声学信号构建拓扑地图，但声呐场所识别天然存在歧义：走廊会产生几乎相同的回波序列，错误回环会导致拓扑地图崩溃。因此需要提升系统的鲁棒性。

Method: BatSLAM 2.0 由三部分构成：更新的声学前端（acoustic front-end）、序列验证器（sequence verifier，用于跟踪并验证回环候选）以及在高性能因子图框架上实现的位姿图（pose graph）。系统在仿真和真实世界记录中均进行了全面评估。

Result: 在仿真与真实场景中，BatSLAM 2.0 均展现出稳健的拓扑地图创建能力，能够抵抗地图崩溃，并具备良好的地图规模可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过引入序列验证与因子图位姿图优化，BatSLAM 2.0 有效缓解了声呐场所识别的歧义问题，实现了仅用声呐的鲁棒 SLAM，适用于黑暗杂乱环境下的导航。

Abstract: Echolocating bats can navigate dark and cluttered spaces using echolocation. Over a decade ago, BatSLAM showed that a robot with a biomimetic binaural sonar can build a topological map of the environment, by recognizing places from the received acoustic signals. Sonar place recognition, however, is ambiguous by nature: corridors produce nearly identical echo trains, and wrong loop closure can collapse the topological map. In this paper, we introduce BatSLAM 2.0, a novel sonar-only SLAM system built from three elements: an updated acoustic front-end, a sequence verifier that tracks and verifies loop closure candidates and a pose graph implemented on a high performance factor graph framework. The system was thoroughly evaluated both in simulated as well as real world recordings. In both cases, the BatSLAM2.0 algorithm shows the capability of robust topological map creation, countering map collapse, and robust scaling of map size.

</details>


### [303] [Passive Stiffness Shaping in Cable-Suspended Aerial Manipulation via Movable Compliant Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40102)
*Antonio Franchi,Amr Afifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将空中飞行器视为可移动的柔性锚点，建立了重力感知的准静态理论，用于预测和塑造缆索悬挂负载的被动笛卡尔刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索悬挂的空中操作具有轻量、适合协同运输与物理交互的架构优势，但负载端所感知的被动机械响应仍缺乏充分理解，也未被系统性地利用。

Method: 将空中飞行器视为可移动的柔性锚点，建立重力感知的准静态理论；适用于任意数量飞行器通过张紧、笔直、不可伸长缆索连接点负载的情形。在选定的重力加载平衡态下，锚点柔性与缆索横向几何柔性在每条腿中串联，而各腿刚度在负载上并联。对于各向同性锚点行为，每条腿精确等效为一根虚拟单侧弹性缆索，并给出由平衡张力支配的轴向—横向刚度分解。由此定义从指令锚点构型到被动负载刚度的非线性映射，其微分可实现通过锚点重定位进行的局部保约束塑造。

Result: 得到轴向—横向刚度分解，并建立从指令锚点构型到被动负载刚度的非线性映射，其微分支持局部保约束的刚度塑造。

Conclusion: 该理论为缆索悬挂空中操作的被动负载刚度预测与塑造提供了框架，并通过动态刚体验证评估其预测在超出解析假设时的适用程度。

Abstract: Cable-suspended aerial manipulation offers a lightweight architecture for cooperative transportation and physical interaction, yet the passive mechanical response perceived at the load remains insufficiently understood and systematically exploited. This work interprets aerial vehicles as movable compliant anchors and develops a gravity-aware quasi-static theory for predicting and shaping the passive Cartesian stiffness of a suspended load. The formulation applies to an arbitrary number of aerial vehicles connected to a point load by taut, straight, inextensible cables. At a selected gravity-loaded equilibrium, aerial-anchor compliance and transverse cable geometric compliance combine in series within each leg, while the leg stiffnesses act in parallel on the load. For isotropic aerial-anchor behavior, each leg is exactly equivalent to a virtual unilateral elastic cable, revealing an axial--transverse stiffness decomposition governed by the equilibrium tension. These results define a nonlinear map from commanded-anchor configuration to passive load stiffness, whose differential enables local constraint-preserving shaping through anchor repositioning. A dynamic rigid-body validation framework with nonlinear vehicle control, elastic-damped tendons, and environmental contact is defined to assess when and to what extent the derived stiffness remains predictive beyond the assumptions of the analytical model.

</details>


### [304] [Tactile Curiosity Drives Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40134)
*Klemens Iten,Alexander Proshkin,Bhavya Sukhija,Stelian Coros,Andreas Krause,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacEx 将触觉反馈融入基于认知不确定性的探索，引导好奇心聚焦触觉通道，从而高效发现接触动力学，支持无环境交互的离线学习与 VLA 模型后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作任务上样本效率低：随机动作采样使大量训练预算浪费在自由空间运动上，远离真正产生操作技能的接触；已有基于模型分歧或认知不确定性的内在动机方法虽优于各向同性噪声，但仍会奖励功能无关的过渡（如自由空间中的乱动）。作者主张触觉反馈是一种自然的探索信号。

Method: 提出 TacEx 框架：把模型不确定性按感知模态分解，并将好奇心导向触觉通道，以触觉锚定探索方向，驱动机器人发现复杂接触动力学；在无任务奖励、无专家示范的条件下学习操作与抓取；利用触觉驱动好奇收集的交互密集数据集支持下游 pick-and-place 策略的离线学习；进一步用触觉驱动探索对 VLA 模型进行后训练。

Result: 触觉驱动的探索使机器人能在无任务奖励与专家示范下发现复杂接触动力学并学会操作和抓取；所收集的交互密集数据集可支持下游 pick-and-place 策略的离线学习，无需额外环境交互；对初始未含触觉反馈的 VLA 模型进行后训练后，下游性能显著提升且保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈可作为有效的探索信号，通过按模态分解不确定性并把好奇心指向触觉通道，TacEx 显著提升了机器人操作技能学习的样本效率，并为离线学习与 VLA 后训练提供了可复用的交互密集数据。

Abstract: Mastering robot manipulation skills via reinforcement learning (RL) remains largely sample-inefficient. The most common RL algorithms rely on random action sampling to discover new strategies, resulting in agents that allocate most of their training budget to motions in free space, away from the contacts from which manipulation skills emerge. Existing intrinsic motivation methods based on model disagreement or epistemic uncertainty improve on isotropic noise, but they can also reward uncertainty in functionally irrelevant transitions, such as erratic motions in free space. In this work, we argue that tactile feedback provides a natural signal for exploration, and introduce TacEx, a framework that incorporates touch into epistemic uncertainty-driven exploration by decomposing model uncertainty across sensory modalities and directing curiosity toward the tactile channel. By anchoring curiosity to the sense of touch, TacEx drives the robot to discover complex contact dynamics, learning to manipulate and grasp objects without task rewards or expert demonstrations during exploration. The interaction-dense dataset collected through this tactile-driven curiosity supports offline learning of downstream pick-and-place policies without additional environment interaction. We further use tactile-driven exploration to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. Although the VLAs are initially pre-trained without tactile feedback, post-training with TacEx substantially improves downstream performance while remaining highly sample-efficient.

</details>


### [305] [Dream4ACT: A Shared Visual Action Interface for Multi-Embodiment Video-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40153)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Jin Xu,Yue Guo,Xin Wu,Yifan Sun,Xiancong Ren,Jianxin Sun,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream4ACT 通过"动作视图"这一共享视觉接口将不同本体的关节配置渲染为多视角图像，实现跨本体的联合视频-动作世界模型，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上取得 88.98% 平均成功率，TriWorldBench 总分 65.66。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机是：视频生成模型（VGM）具有强大的时空先验，可用于具身智能的观测-动作建模，但动作空间中的关节向量缺乏显式的图像空间结构，且在不同本体间维度与语义各异，难以直接利用 VGM 的时空先验；而末端执行器可视化虽提供了替代方案，却无法指定机器人执行所需的完整关节构型。因此需要一个能跨本体统一动作表示的方案。

Method: 提出 Dream4ACT 世界模型：(1) 引入共享视觉动作接口"动作视图"（action views），基于 URDF 正运动学从四个预设虚拟相机渲染目标关节配置，保留本体的关节几何特性，同时让观测与动作序列共享同一视频自编码器与扩散 Transformer；(2) 通过掩码流匹配（masked flow-matching），在单一联合训练模型中通过改变被破坏的未来序列，支持前向动力学、逆向动力学以及联合观测-动作生成；(3) 提出免训练、URDF 约束的多视角恢复机制，从预测的动作视图中恢复可执行的动作序列，无需依赖学习到的本体特定解码器。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上取得 88.98% 的平均成功率，在 TriWorldBench 上取得 65.66 的总体分数，支持有效的闭环操作，并通过视觉动作接口实现了具有竞争力的动作条件多视角预测。

Conclusion: Dream4ACT 利用共享视觉动作接口统一了跨本体的动作表示，使视频生成模型的时空先验能直接用于具身观测-动作建模，结合免训练的 URDF 约束恢复机制实现了闭环操作，验证了视觉动作接口在跨本体世界建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Video generation models (VGMs) offer strong spatiotemporal priors for embodied observation--action modeling. However, joint-space action vectors lack explicit image-space structure and vary in dimensionality and semantics across embodiments, making it challenging to directly leverage the rich spatiotemporal priors of VGMs. End-effector visualizations provide an alternative but do not specify the full articulated configuration needed for robot execution. We present Dream4ACT, a world model built for joint video-action modeling across embodiments. To unify action representations across embodiments, we introduce a shared visual action interface, called action views, which render target joint configurations from four prescribed virtual cameras using URDF-based forward kinematics. This shared visual representation preserves embodiment-specific articulated geometry while allowing observation and action sequences to share a video autoencoder and diffusion transformer. Through masked flow-matching, our model supports forward dynamics, inverse dynamics, and joint observation--action generation within a single jointly trained model by varying which future sequences are corrupted. To recover executable action sequences from predicted action views, we propose a training-free, URDF-constrained multiview recovery mechanism, without a learned embodiment-specific decoder. Dream4ACT achieves an average success rate of 88.98\% on RoboTwin~2.0 and an overall score of 65.66 on TriWorldBench, supporting effective closed-loop manipulation and competitive action-conditioned multiview prediction through the visual action interface.

</details>


### [306] [Rethinking Legibility in Social Robot Hallway Navigation: Impact of Intent Representation and Human Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40158)
*Pranav Goyal,Andrew Stratton,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究社交导航中机器人可读性动作的生成，通过两项用户实验探究意图表征方式与行人注意力水平对导航表现和人类印象的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可读性动作生成框架多针对静态观察者，而社交机器人导航面临动态行人环境中人类注意力分散的新挑战，需要机器人在保证安全的同时清晰传达意图。

Method: 聚焦走廊导航场景，在共享的模型预测控制（MPC）框架内实现多种可读性公式，开展两项受控用户研究。研究1（N=45）考察意图表征的作用，对比基于通过侧、基于目的地及非可读基线；研究2（N=45）考察行人注意力的影响。

Result: 研究1表明，基于通过侧的可读性（尤其是自适应更新时）使人类动作更平滑，并被感知为更有能力、心理和身体负担更低。研究2表明，即使在注意力分散下，可读性动作仍能带来平滑的人类动作，尽管这未一致反映在主观评分中。

Conclusion: 社交机器人导航中有效的可读性动作受益于交互层面的意图表征以支持协调，且部分效果在人类注意力分散时依然存在。代码已开源。

Abstract: We focus on legible robot motion generation in social navigation settings. Legibility in human-robot interaction (HRI) is often described as the property of robot motion that enables an observer to confidently infer the robot's intent. While mature frameworks exist for generating legible motion in front of static observers, social robot navigation presents a new challenge: the robot must clearly convey its intent while ensuring human safety in dynamic pedestrian environments where human attention is often divided. With the goal of enabling robots to generate legible motion in dynamic and constrained spaces, we investigate how the choice of representation and the level of human attention shape navigation performance and human impressions. Focusing on the ubiquitous and demanding scenario of hallway navigation, we conduct two controlled user studies involving alternative legibility formulations implemented within a shared model predictive control framework. Study 1 (N = 45) investigates the role of intent representation, showing that passing-side legibility, particularly when adaptively updated, leads to smoother human motion and is perceived as more competent and less mentally and physically demanding than destination-based and non-legible baselines. Study 2 (N = 45) examines the effect of pedestrian attention, demonstrating that legible motion allows for smooth human motion even under distraction, even if this is not consistently reflected in subjective ratings. Together, these findings suggest that effective legible motion in social robot navigation benefits from interaction-level intent representations that support coordination, with some effects persisting even when human attention is divided. Code is available at https://github.com/fluentrobotics/Legible_MPPI.

</details>


### [307] [PrefPI: Preference-Guided Steering into Out-of-Distribution Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40165)
*Seungeun Rho,Wontaek Kim,Danfei Xu,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefPI：用相对偏好引导预训练生成式机器人策略，实现超越初始策略能力范围的行为迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习方法主要在策略已能生成的行为模式内做“锐化”，难以获得初始策略极少或从未出现的目标行为。本文关注“超越初始有效支撑域”的策略引导问题。

Method: 将偏好学习表述为偏好条件生成建模：偏好轨迹定义条件分布，其与更广行为先验的密度比提供隐式偏好信号，并用无分类器引导（CFG）放大；反复进行偏好条件建模与引导，形成偏好引导的策略迭代。方法在扩散策略与 PI0.5 流匹配 VLA 上验证（仿真与真实世界）。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中均产生显著行为改变，且所需反馈有限。真实硬件上仅用 150 条偏好标注轨迹，即将物体搬运高度从 10.7 cm 提升至 19.8 cm。

Conclusion: 偏好条件生成建模结合 CFG 可作为一种策略迭代机制，在少量相对偏好反馈下引导预训练生成式机器人策略进入初始支撑域之外的新行为。

Abstract: We present PrefPI (Preference-Guided Policy Iteration), an iterative framework for steering pretrained generative robot policies using only relative preferences over self-generated trajectories. Unlike prior preference-learning methods that primarily sharpen modes already represented by the policy, we study steering beyond the initial effective support, where desired behaviors are rarely or never observed under the initial policy. Our key idea is to formulate preference learning as preference-conditioned generative modeling: preferred trajectories define a conditional distribution, whose density ratio with the broader behavior prior provides an implicit preference signal amplified by classifier-free guidance (CFG). Repeating this preference-conditioned modeling and guidance step yields a form of preference-guided policy iteration, turning incremental improvements toward previously inaccessible behaviors. Across diffusion policies and the PI0.5 flow- matching VLA in simulation and the real world, PrefPI produces substantial behavioral shifts with limited feedback. In particular, PrefPI increases object transport height from 10.7 cm to 19.8 cm on real hardware with only 150 preference-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [308] [Social-WM: Safety-Aware Latent World Models for Robot Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40177)
*Zhihao Zheng,Mooi Choo Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Social-WM是一个从自我中心RGB视频训练的高效潜在世界模型规划框架，通过"标称动作-可执行动作"差异提供安全性信号，实现社交导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航不仅需要预测动作的未来后果，还需判断标称动作在周围物理与社交约束下是否真正可执行。现有方法往往依赖显式行人追踪、特权人类状态或在线强化学习，成本高且泛化性受限，因此需要一种能直接从经验中学习安全相关后果的高效规划框架。

Method: 提出Social-WM：基于自我中心RGB视频序列训练潜在世界模型。核心观察是社交导航经验中存在标称动作与可执行动作的系统性差异（如朝行人/障碍物前进时标称前向动作需被约束）。方法通过动作条件化的未来预测学习安全相关后果，预测目标为每条指令后实际观测到的未来；并引入"可实现逆动力学"目标，将观测到的潜在状态转移与真正实现的动作（而非标称动作）关联。部署时，候选动作在潜在世界模型中"想象"，由逆动力学模型估计其可实现性，标称-可实现差异作为执行前的安全信号。

Result: 在Social-HM3D上达到63.77%成功率，同时将人类碰撞降低至21.67%；在零样本迁移到Social-MP3D时仍保持较强性能。无需显式行人追踪、特权人类状态或在线强化学习。

Conclusion: 所学动力学与可实现性模型与目标无关，同时支持位置目标与图像目标导航，验证了从自我中心视频中学习潜在世界模型与可实现性建模在安全社交导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires a robot to anticipate not only the future consequences of its actions, but also whether a nominal action can actually be executed under surrounding physical and social constraints. We present Social-WM, an efficient latent world-model planning framework trained from egocentric RGB video sequences. Our key observation is that social-navigation experience contains a systematic discrepancy between the nominal action and the realizable action: a nominal forward action may be fully executed in free space, but needs to be constrained when heading towards a pedestrian or obstacle. Social-WM learns these safety-relevant consequences directly through action-conditioned future prediction, where the target is the actual observed future following each command. We further introduce a realizable inverse-dynamics objective that associates observed latent transitions with the action actually realized rather than the nominal one. At deployment, candidate actions are imagined through the latent world model, and the inverse dynamics model estimates their realizability; nominal--realizable discrepancy then provides a safety signal before execution. The learned dynamics and realizability model remain goal-independent and support both position- and image-goal navigation. On Social-HM3D, Social-WM achieves 63.77% success while reducing human collisions to 21.67%, and maintains strong performance under zero-shot transfer to Social-MP3D, without explicit pedestrian tracking, privileged human state, or online reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [309] [Non-Invasive Inspection of Water Canals Using Dronar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40178)
*Michael Zielinski,Zhizhan Wang,Benjamin Dymond,Reza Razavian,Zhongwang Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出并验证了一种无需排水的运河河床检测方法"dronar"，将消费级声呐集成到无人水面艇（USV）上，在亚利桑那运河的三次现场测试中成功识别了泥沙淤积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放混凝土运河是重要的输水基础设施，但长期使用会出现衬砌变形、混凝土开裂和河床泥沙淤积等问题。目前仅在每四年的干涸周期才能检测，过程资源消耗大，导致维护团队无法优先处理受损最严重的渠段。因此需要一种可轻松部署、非侵入性且无需排水的检测方法。

Method: 研究团队开发了"dronar"系统，将消费级声呐集成到无人水面艇（USV）上，使声呐换能器位于运河水面以下，并结合低成本现成无人机与声呐技术。在凤凰城亚利桑那运河进行了三次现场测试，采用DownScan深度剖面进行检测。

Result: 淤积渠段的DownScan深度剖面持续比同一渠段清理后的剖面更浅，沿轨迹观察到最大约15 cm的偏移。在清洁渠段上重复运行产生的DownScan深度剖面高度重叠，证实了dronar在河床自然变化条件下具有可重复的测量能力。

Conclusion: dronar系统被确立为一种可行的概念验证工具，可用于非侵入式运河河床检测，有助于在不排水的情况下识别淤积问题，支持维护工作的优先级排序。

Abstract: Open concrete canals play a vital role in water transportation, serving as primary water infrastructure for millions of people across the Phoenix, Arizona, metro area. Over time, the concrete canals can experience a range of issues, including canal lining deformation, cracked concrete, and sediment buildup on the canal floor. Identifying such critical issues is a resource-intensive process, which currently happens only during four-year dry-up cycles. This prevents the maintenance crew from prioritizing operations on the most affected canal segments. To address this issue, the research team has developed and verified an easily deployable and non-invasive method to inspect canal beds without draining the water. This inspection system integrates affordable, off-the-shelf drone and sonar technology (termed dronar). This dronar system includes a consumer-grade sonar system integrated into an unmanned surface vehicle (USV) that carries the sonar transducer just under the surface of the canal water. This paper presents a proof-of-concept demonstration of the dronar system across three field tests on the Arizona Canal in Phoenix. DownScan depth profiles from the sedimented canal segment were consistently shallower than profiles from the same segment after cleaning, with offsets of up to 15 cm observed along the track. Repeated runs over the clean segment produced closely overlapping DownScan depth profiles, confirming that the dronar yields repeatable measurements across the natural variation of the canal bed. These results establish the dronar as a viable proof-of-concept tool for non-invasive canal bed inspection.

</details>


### [310] [Centralized Multi-UAV Exploration and 3D Reconstruction Using Single-UAV Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40208)
*João Félix Mendes,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集中式多无人机探索框架，通过共享全局TSDF地图与集中式规划，使现有单机采样规划器可直接用于多机场景，并集成机间避碰与自过滤机制。在仿真中使用四种采样规划器于两种部署配置下评估，发现分离式部署（SS）在探索速度与覆盖率上均一致优于联合式部署（JS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单无人机探索方法扩展到多无人机团队可提升覆盖速度与鲁棒性，但会带来一致建图、安全导航与部署策略等挑战。本文旨在复现单机方法优势的同时解决多机扩展问题，并探究部署策略对探索性能的影响。

Method: 提出集中式多无人机探索框架：基于voxblox改造建图流程，实时融合多机深度测量到共享全局TSDF地图；采用集中式规划，保留四种现有采样规划器（RH-NBVP、KRH-NBVP、AEP、KAEP）的核心采样逻辑，仅做系统级适配；并集成机间避碰与机器人自过滤机制，避免将其他无人机重建为静态障碍。

Result: 在多环境仿真下，对比两种部署配置：联合起始（JS，无人机初始位置相近）与分离起始（SS，无人机初始位置分散）。结果显示SS部署在所有规划器下均一致实现更快的探索速度和更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该集中式框架能让现有单机采样规划器有效服务于多机探索，且部署策略对多无人机探索性能具有重要影响，分离式部署优于联合式部署。

Abstract: Extending single Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) exploration methods to multi-UAV teams can improve coverage speed and robustness, but introduces challenges such as consistent mapping, safe navigation, and deployment strategy. In this work, we present a centralized multi-UAV exploration framework that enables the use of existing single-UAV sampling-based planners in a multi-UAV setting.
  The proposed architecture allows multiple UAVs to collaboratively explore unknown environments using a shared global Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF) map and centralized planning. Building on the voxblox library, we adapt its mapping pipeline to support real-time fusion of depth measurements from multiple UAVs into a common TSDF representation. In addition, inter-UAV collision avoidance and robot self-filtering mechanisms are integrated into the system to ensure safe navigation and prevent reconstruction of other UAVs as static obstacles.
  The framework is evaluated in simulation using four sampling-based exploration planners - RH-NBVP, KRH-NBVP, AEP, and KAEP - whose core sampling logic is preserved, with only system-level adaptations for multi-UAV operation. Experiments are conducted across multiple environments and under two deployment configurations: Joint Start (JS), where UAVs are initialized in close proximity, and Separated Start (SS), where UAVs are initialized in distinct locations. Results show that SS deployments consistently achieve faster exploration and improved coverage across all planners, highlighting the importance of the deployment strategy in multi-UAV exploration performance.

</details>


### [311] [STARS: From Spatiotemporal Dynamics to Social Representations in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40245)
*Nathan Tsoi,Michael J. Munje,Tejas Oberoi,Rishab Maheshwari,Pengen Zheng,Tanush Chauhan,Peter Stone,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SocialNav-SUB——一个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在真实社交机器人导航场景中场景理解能力的 VQA 数据集与基准，发现当前最先进 VLM 虽有一定表现，但仍不及简单规则方法与人类共识基线，揭示其在社交场景理解上的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航需要在动态、以人为中心的环境中做出符合社会规范的决策，依赖于鲁棒的场景理解。VLM 具备物体识别、常识推理与上下文理解等能力，看似契合社交导航需求，但其能否准确理解复杂社交导航场景（如推断智能体间时空关系与人类意图）尚不明确，且现有工作缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 构建 SocialNav-SUB（Social Navigation Scene Understanding Benchmark），一个面向真实社交机器人导航场景的视觉问答（VQA）数据集与基准，提供统一框架，在需要空间、时空与社交推理的 VQA 任务上，将 VLM 与人类基线及规则基线进行对比评估，并对当前最先进 VLM 开展实验。

Result: 表现最好的 VLM 在与人类答案一致的概率上取得较好结果，但仍逊于更简单的规则方法与人类共识基线，表明当前 VLM 在社交场景理解上存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 该基准为社交机器人导航的基础模型研究奠定基础，提供探索如何定制 VLM 以满足真实社交导航需求的框架。论文概览、代码与数据见 https://larg.github.io/socialnav-sub。

Abstract: Robot navigation in dynamic, human-centered environments requires socially-compliant decisions grounded in robust scene understanding. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit promising capabilities such as object recognition, common-sense reasoning, and contextual understanding, capabilities that align with the nuanced requirements of social robot navigation.
